练习 2close_neg_lt定理附录解答

四条序的换形

theorem close_neg_lt (x L eps : ratRep) (h : close x L eps) :
    ratLtRep (ratSubRep L x) eps :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

见证 d 满足 L - x ≤ d < eps；前一条由 ratAbsRel_neg_le 与 ratNegRep_sub 换形得到，再与 d < eps 接力。

提示 2目标形态

close x L eps → (L - x) < eps。

提示 3关键件

Exists.elim · ratAbsRel_neg_le · ratNegRep_sub · ratLtRep_of_le_lt。

查看解答 定理 · 11 行

close x L eps 的负侧是严格的：见证 d 满足 L - x ≤ d < eps。 正侧的严格版本可以直接从 ratAbsRel_le 得到，负侧要先过 ratAbsRel_neg_le 与 ratNegRep_sub 的换形。

theorem close_neg_lt (x L eps : ratRep) (h : close x L eps) :
    ratLtRep (ratSubRep L x) eps :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (d : ratRep)
        (hd : ratAbsRel (ratSubRep x L) d ∧ ratLtRep d eps) =>
      ratLtRep_of_le_lt (ratSubRep L x) d eps
        (ratLeRep_congr_gen
          (ratNegRep (ratSubRep x L)) (ratSubRep L x) d d
          (ratNegRep_sub x L) (ratRel_refl d)
          (ratAbsRel_neg_le (ratSubRep x L) d (And.left hd)))
        (And.right hd))
练习 3ratLtRep_add_right定理附录解答
theorem ratLtRep_add_right (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep x y) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

弱序交给 ratLeRep_add_right；严格性用加法消去，若两边相加后相等就得到原两项相等，撞上严格序的第二分量。

提示 2目标形态

x < y → x + z < y + z。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_add_right · ratLeRep_add_cancel_gen · ratLtRep_right。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行

严格序对右加同一个数稳定。弱序是 ratLeRep_add_right；严格性用 加法消去：若两边相加后相等，消去公共加数就得到原来的两项相等， 与严格序的第二分量冲突。

theorem ratLtRep_add_right (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep x y) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z) :=
  And.intro
    (ratLeRep_add_right x y z (ratLtRep_le x y h))
    (fun (hxy : ratRel (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z)) =>
      ratLtRep_right x y h (ratRel_add_cancel_gen x y z hxy))
练习 4ratLtRep_congr_fst定理附录解答
theorem ratLtRep_congr_fst (a b c : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep b c) (hab : ratRel a b) : ratLtRep a c :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

弱序沿 ratRel 把左端点换成新的代表元；不相等部分把等价关系反着串一次。

提示 2目标形态

b < c → a ~ b → a < c。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_congr_gen · ratRel_symm · ratRel_trans_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 7 行

严格序只改写左端点：弱序沿 ratRel 搬运，不相等部分把等价关系 反着串一次。

theorem ratLtRep_congr_fst (a b c : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep b c) (hab : ratRel a b) : ratLtRep a c :=
  And.intro
    (ratLeRep_congr_gen b a c c (ratRel_symm a b hab) (ratRel_refl c)
      (ratLtRep_le b c h))
    (fun (hac : ratRel a c) =>
      ratLtRep_right b c h (ratRel_trans_gen b a c (ratRel_symm a b hab) hac))
练习 5ratLtRep_of_sub_pos定理附录解答
theorem ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (x y : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratSubRep x y)) : ratLtRep y x :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

两边同加 y；(x - y) + y 收回 x，0 + y 收回 y，得到 y < x。

提示 2目标形态

0 < x - y → y < x。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_add_right · ratSubRep_add_self · ratAdd_zero_left。

查看解答 定理 · 8 行

0 < x - y 反过来读成 y < x：两边同加 y，左边用 (x - y) + y ~ x 收回，右边用左零元收回。

theorem ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (x y : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratSubRep x y)) : ratLtRep y x :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd y (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x y) y) x
    (ratLtRep_congr_fst
      y (ratAddRep ratZeroRep y) (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x y) y)
      (ratLtRep_add_right ratZeroRep (ratSubRep x y) y h)
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep ratZeroRep y) y (ratAdd_zero_left y)))
    (ratSubRep_add_self x y)
练习 6positiveNear_of_continuousAt定理附录解答

连续给出邻域里的正号

theorem positiveNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f a)) :
    PositiveNear f a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

取 ε = f a；负侧严格界给出 f a - f x < f a，两边加 f x 得 f a < f a + f x，再把左端收回 f x。

提示 2目标形态

PositiveNear f a。

提示 3关键件

close_neg_lt · ratLtRep_add_right · ratLtRep_congr_fst · ratLtRep_sub_pos · ratAdd_sub_self。

查看解答 定理 · 22 行

取 ε = f a。close_neg_lt 给出 f a - f x < f a；两边加 f x 得 f a < f a + f x，再用 (f a + f x) - f a ~ f x 把左端收回 f x。

theorem positiveNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f a)) :
    PositiveNear f a :=
  Exists.elim (hf (f a) h)
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → close (f x) (f a) (f a)) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
            ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep
              (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (f a) (f x)) (f a)) (f x)
              (ratLtRep_sub_pos (ratAddRep (f a) (f x)) (f a)
                (ratLtRep_congr_fst
                  (f a)
                  (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f a) (f x)) (f x))
                  (ratAddRep (f a) (f x))
                  (ratLtRep_add_right (ratSubRep (f a) (f x)) (f a) (f x)
                    (close_neg_lt (f x) (f a) (f a) ((And.right hd) x hx)))
                  (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f a) (f x)) (f x))
                    (f a) (ratSubRep_add_self (f a) (f x)))))
              (ratAdd_sub_self (f a) (f x)))))
练习 7negativeNear_of_continuousAt定理附录解答

连续给出邻域里的负号

theorem negativeNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

取 ε = -f a；这次走正侧 ratAbsRel_le，f x - f a < -f a，两边加 f a 后左端收回 f x、右端收成 0。

提示 2目标形态

∃ delta > 0, ∀ x, close x a delta → f x < 0。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_pos_of_neg · ratAbsRel_le · ratLtRep_add_right · ratAdd_neg_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 32 行

取 ε = -f a，它是正的。这次用正侧 ratAbsRel_le：f x - f a < -f a， 两边加 f a 后左端收回 f x，右端用逆元律收成 0。

theorem negativeNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  Exists.elim (hf (ratNegRep (f a)) (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg (f a) h))
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → close (f x) (f a) (ratNegRep (f a))) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
            ratLtRep_congr_snd (f x) (ratAddRep (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a))
              ratZeroRep
              (ratLtRep_congr_fst
                (f x)
                (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) (f a))
                (ratAddRep (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a))
                (ratLtRep_add_right (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a)
                  (Exists.elim ((And.right hd) x hx)
                    (fun (d : ratRep)
                        (hd2 : ratAbsRel (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) d ∧
                          ratLtRep d (ratNegRep (f a))) =>
                      ratLtRep_of_le_lt (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) d
                        (ratNegRep (f a))
                        (ratAbsRel_le (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) d (And.left hd2))
                        (And.right hd2))))
                (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) (f a))
                  (f x) (ratSubRep_add_self (f x) (f a))))
              (ratRel_trans_gen (ratAddRep (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a))
                (ratAddRep (f a) (ratNegRep (f a))) ratZeroRep
                (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a))
                (ratAdd_neg_gen (f a))))))
练习 8neZeroNear_of_continuousAt定理附录解答

邻域里不为零

theorem neZeroNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ¬ ratRel (f a) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

序的全性把 f a 分到正、负两支，各用上一条定理；「严格正/负」的第二分量就是「不等于零」。

提示 2目标形态

¬ f a ~ 0 → ∃ delta > 0, ∀ x, close x a delta → ¬ f x ~ 0。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_total · ratRel_symm · ratLtRep_right · 上两条定理。

查看解答 定理 · 33 行

f a 不为零，序的全性把它分到「正」与「负」两支；两支各自用上一条 定理，再把「严格正/负」读成「不等于零」。

theorem neZeroNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ¬ ratRel (f a) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (h0 : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (f a)) =>
      Exists.elim
        (positiveNear_of_continuousAt f a hf
          (And.intro h0
            (fun (h0a : ratRel ratZeroRep (f a)) =>
              h (ratRel_symm ratZeroRep (f a) h0a))))
        (fun (delta : ratRep)
            (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
              ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta →
                ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f x)) =>
          Exists.intro delta
            (And.intro (And.left hd)
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
                (fun (hx0 : ratRel (f x) ratZeroRep) =>
                  (ratLtRep_right ratZeroRep (f x) ((And.right hd) x hx))
                    (ratRel_symm (f x) ratZeroRep hx0))))))
    (fun (h0 : ratLeRep (f a) ratZeroRep) =>
      Exists.elim
        (negativeNear_of_continuousAt f a hf (And.intro h0 h))
        (fun (delta : ratRep)
            (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
              ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta →
                ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep) =>
          Exists.intro delta
            (And.intro (And.left hd)
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
                (ratLtRep_right (f x) ratZeroRep ((And.right hd) x hx))))))
    (ratLeRep_total ratZeroRep (f a))
练习 9continuousAt_lt_of_lt定理附录解答

严格不等式的稳定性

theorem continuousAt_lt_of_lt (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (hg : ContinuousAt g a)
    (h : ratLtRep (f a) (g a)) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep (f x) (g x) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

差 g - f 连续、在 a 处为正，保号性给出邻域里的正差，再读回 f x < g x。

提示 2目标形态

f a < g a → ∃ delta > 0, ∀ x, close x a delta → f x < g x。

提示 3关键件

continuousAt_sub · ratLtRep_sub_pos · positiveNear_of_continuousAt · ratLtRep_of_sub_pos。

查看解答 定理 · 17 行

差 g - f 在 a 处连续，且 g a - f a > 0；保号性给出邻域里的正差， 再把正差读回 f x < g x。

theorem continuousAt_lt_of_lt (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (hg : ContinuousAt g a)
    (h : ratLtRep (f a) (g a)) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep (f x) (g x) :=
  Exists.elim
    (positiveNear_of_continuousAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (g x) (f x)) a
      (continuousAt_sub g f a hg hf)
      (ratLtRep_sub_pos (g a) (f a) h))
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta →
            ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratSubRep (g x) (f x))) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
            ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (g x) (f x) ((And.right hd) x hx))))

局部保号：连续函数在一点附近不变号

11 道练习 · 11 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · positiveNear_iff 定理
theorem positiveNear_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep) :
    PositiveNear f a ↔
      ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f x) :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : PositiveNear f a) => h)
    (fun (h : ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f x)) => h)
练习 2 · close_neg_lt 定理

close x L eps 的负侧是严格的：见证 d 满足 L - x ≤ d < eps。 正侧的严格版本可以直接从 ratAbsRel_le 得到，负侧要先过 ratAbsRel_neg_le 与 ratNegRep_sub 的换形。

theorem close_neg_lt (x L eps : ratRep) (h : close x L eps) :
    ratLtRep (ratSubRep L x) eps :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (d : ratRep)
        (hd : ratAbsRel (ratSubRep x L) d ∧ ratLtRep d eps) =>
      ratLtRep_of_le_lt (ratSubRep L x) d eps
        (ratLeRep_congr_gen
          (ratNegRep (ratSubRep x L)) (ratSubRep L x) d d
          (ratNegRep_sub x L) (ratRel_refl d)
          (ratAbsRel_neg_le (ratSubRep x L) d (And.left hd)))
        (And.right hd))
练习 3 · ratLtRep_add_right 定理

严格序对右加同一个数稳定。弱序是 ratLeRep_add_right；严格性用 加法消去：若两边相加后相等，消去公共加数就得到原来的两项相等， 与严格序的第二分量冲突。

theorem ratLtRep_add_right (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep x y) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z) :=
  And.intro
    (ratLeRep_add_right x y z (ratLtRep_le x y h))
    (fun (hxy : ratRel (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z)) =>
      ratLtRep_right x y h (ratRel_add_cancel_gen x y z hxy))
练习 4 · ratLtRep_congr_fst 定理

严格序只改写左端点：弱序沿 ratRel 搬运，不相等部分把等价关系 反着串一次。

theorem ratLtRep_congr_fst (a b c : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep b c) (hab : ratRel a b) : ratLtRep a c :=
  And.intro
    (ratLeRep_congr_gen b a c c (ratRel_symm a b hab) (ratRel_refl c)
      (ratLtRep_le b c h))
    (fun (hac : ratRel a c) =>
      ratLtRep_right b c h (ratRel_trans_gen b a c (ratRel_symm a b hab) hac))
练习 5 · ratLtRep_of_sub_pos 定理

0 < x - y 反过来读成 y < x：两边同加 y，左边用 (x - y) + y ~ x 收回，右边用左零元收回。

theorem ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (x y : ratRep)
    (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratSubRep x y)) : ratLtRep y x :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd y (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x y) y) x
    (ratLtRep_congr_fst
      y (ratAddRep ratZeroRep y) (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x y) y)
      (ratLtRep_add_right ratZeroRep (ratSubRep x y) y h)
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep ratZeroRep y) y (ratAdd_zero_left y)))
    (ratSubRep_add_self x y)
练习 6 · positiveNear_of_continuousAt 定理

取 ε = f a。close_neg_lt 给出 f a - f x < f a；两边加 f x 得 f a < f a + f x，再用 (f a + f x) - f a ~ f x 把左端收回 f x。

theorem positiveNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f a)) :
    PositiveNear f a :=
  Exists.elim (hf (f a) h)
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → close (f x) (f a) (f a)) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
            ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep
              (ratSubRep (ratAddRep (f a) (f x)) (f a)) (f x)
              (ratLtRep_sub_pos (ratAddRep (f a) (f x)) (f a)
                (ratLtRep_congr_fst
                  (f a)
                  (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f a) (f x)) (f x))
                  (ratAddRep (f a) (f x))
                  (ratLtRep_add_right (ratSubRep (f a) (f x)) (f a) (f x)
                    (close_neg_lt (f x) (f a) (f a) ((And.right hd) x hx)))
                  (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f a) (f x)) (f x))
                    (f a) (ratSubRep_add_self (f a) (f x)))))
              (ratAdd_sub_self (f a) (f x)))))
练习 7 · negativeNear_of_continuousAt 定理

取 ε = -f a，它是正的。这次用正侧 ratAbsRel_le：f x - f a < -f a， 两边加 f a 后左端收回 f x，右端用逆元律收成 0。

theorem negativeNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  Exists.elim (hf (ratNegRep (f a)) (ratLtRep_pos_of_neg (f a) h))
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → close (f x) (f a) (ratNegRep (f a))) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
            ratLtRep_congr_snd (f x) (ratAddRep (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a))
              ratZeroRep
              (ratLtRep_congr_fst
                (f x)
                (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) (f a))
                (ratAddRep (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a))
                (ratLtRep_add_right (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a)
                  (Exists.elim ((And.right hd) x hx)
                    (fun (d : ratRep)
                        (hd2 : ratAbsRel (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) d ∧
                          ratLtRep d (ratNegRep (f a))) =>
                      ratLtRep_of_le_lt (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) d
                        (ratNegRep (f a))
                        (ratAbsRel_le (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) d (And.left hd2))
                        (And.right hd2))))
                (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep (f x) (f a)) (f a))
                  (f x) (ratSubRep_add_self (f x) (f a))))
              (ratRel_trans_gen (ratAddRep (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a))
                (ratAddRep (f a) (ratNegRep (f a))) ratZeroRep
                (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep (f a)) (f a))
                (ratAdd_neg_gen (f a))))))
练习 8 · neZeroNear_of_continuousAt 定理

f a 不为零，序的全性把它分到「正」与「负」两支；两支各自用上一条 定理，再把「严格正/负」读成「不等于零」。

theorem neZeroNear_of_continuousAt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (h : ¬ ratRel (f a) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ¬ ratRel (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (h0 : ratLeRep ratZeroRep (f a)) =>
      Exists.elim
        (positiveNear_of_continuousAt f a hf
          (And.intro h0
            (fun (h0a : ratRel ratZeroRep (f a)) =>
              h (ratRel_symm ratZeroRep (f a) h0a))))
        (fun (delta : ratRep)
            (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
              ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta →
                ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f x)) =>
          Exists.intro delta
            (And.intro (And.left hd)
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
                (fun (hx0 : ratRel (f x) ratZeroRep) =>
                  (ratLtRep_right ratZeroRep (f x) ((And.right hd) x hx))
                    (ratRel_symm (f x) ratZeroRep hx0))))))
    (fun (h0 : ratLeRep (f a) ratZeroRep) =>
      Exists.elim
        (negativeNear_of_continuousAt f a hf (And.intro h0 h))
        (fun (delta : ratRep)
            (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
              ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta →
                ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep) =>
          Exists.intro delta
            (And.intro (And.left hd)
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
                (ratLtRep_right (f x) ratZeroRep ((And.right hd) x hx))))))
    (ratLeRep_total ratZeroRep (f a))
练习 9 · continuousAt_lt_of_lt 定理

差 g - f 在 a 处连续，且 g a - f a > 0；保号性给出邻域里的正差， 再把正差读回 f x < g x。

theorem continuousAt_lt_of_lt (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (a : ratRep)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f a) (hg : ContinuousAt g a)
    (h : ratLtRep (f a) (g a)) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep (f x) (g x) :=
  Exists.elim
    (positiveNear_of_continuousAt (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (g x) (f x)) a
      (continuousAt_sub g f a hg hf)
      (ratLtRep_sub_pos (g a) (f a) h))
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta →
            ratLtRep ratZeroRep (ratSubRep (g x) (f x))) =>
      Exists.intro delta
        (And.intro (And.left hd)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x a delta) =>
            ratLtRep_of_sub_pos (g x) (f x) ((And.right hd) x hx))))
练习 10 · demo_positiveNear_id 定理
theorem demo_positiveNear_id (a : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep ratZeroRep a) :
    PositiveNear (fun (x : ratRep) => x) a :=
  positiveNear_of_continuousAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) a
    (continuousAt_id a) h
练习 11 · demo_negativeNear_id 定理

恒等函数在负点附近为负。

theorem demo_negativeNear_id (a : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep a ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), close x a delta → ratLtRep x ratZeroRep :=
  negativeNear_of_continuousAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) a
    (continuousAt_id a) h

III.6 · 本章第 11 / 16 单元 · 全书第 134 / 171 单元

134闭区间上的最值：上确界与达到

Unit 134 — Extreme Values on a Closed Interval: The Supremum Must Be Attained

5 个定义 · 21 个定理 · 16 道练习 · 48 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

连续函数在闭区间上取到最大值，这是分析里第一个「存在性」结论：它不是从定义直接读出来的，而是先取像的上确界，再证明这个上确界被某个点取到。前一半只依赖序，后一半才是真正的分析内容。

把两半分开写，结构就清楚了。设 A 是区间里的点，f 的像指所有 f x 的等价类。若像有上确界 M，并且有 c ∈ A 使 f c ~ M，那么 M 就是最大值：上界给出 f x ≤ M，而 f c ~ M 说明 M 本身在像里。反过来，若最大值存在，它的值就是像的上确界。于是「最大值存在」与「像的上确界存在且被取到」是同一件事；连续性的全部工作，都花在证明后一句的后半段上。

这一步在 ℚ 上会断。像的上确界可能是一个无理数，因而没有任何有理点取到它；要把它写成一个具体反例，需要乘法与序的相容性，以及 2 没有有理平方根这条数论事实。课程库还没有这两样，所以这里先把「上确界被取到」留作假设，等 ℝ 层的完备性接上以后再用。

能立刻证明的充分条件是单调。f 在 [a,b] 上不降时，右端点 b 就是最大值：每个 x ≤ b，单调性直接把 f x 压到 f b 以下；左端点 a 同理给出最小值。这条定理不需要完备性，也不需要乘法，是闭区间上最值问题的一块干净样本。

闭区间三个字不能省。把 [0,1] 换成开区间 (0,1)，恒等函数就没有最大值：任取 c ∈ (0,1)，c + (1-c)/2 仍落在区间里，而且比 c 大。取中点是构造性的，只用加法和减法，正好说明「端点闭」在最大值的论证里承担了什么。

边界：函数取 ratRep → ratRep，序取有理代表元上的 ratLeRep；不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。连续函数的无条件最值定理留到完备性一章。

定义：区间、像、最大值与单调

闭区间的成员谓词：两端都取非严格序。把它写成谓词而不是集合，后面的上界与上确界就能直接套 lib/Order 里的定义。

定义InClosed声明 1
def InClosed (a b x : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ratLeRep a x ∧ ratLeRep x b

像里的一个值：存在 A 中的点，其函数值与 y 代表元等价。用 ratRel 而不是字面相等，是因为函数的输出本来就只在商的意义下比较。

定义ImageOn声明 2
def ImageOn (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop) (y : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∃ (x : ratRep), A x ∧ ratRel (f x) y

「M 是 f 在 A 上的最大值」分两件事：M 确实被某点取到，且 M 压住所有函数值。两件事缺一不可：只有上界不知道是否取到，只有取到不知道是不是最大。

定义IsMaxOn声明 3
def IsMaxOn (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop) (M : ratRep) : Prop :=
  (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) ∧
    ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep (f x) M

「f 在 A 上取到最大值」：有一个点，它的函数值压住 A 上所有函数值。

定义AttainsMaxOn声明 4
def AttainsMaxOn (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop) : Prop :=
  ∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep (f x) (f c)

闭区间上的单调不降：只比较区间里的点，所以量词带着两个成员前提。

定义NondecreasingOn声明 5
def NondecreasingOn (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x y : ratRep), InClosed a b x → InClosed a b y →
    ratLeRep x y → ratLeRep (f x) (f y)

定义的展开形式与投影

定义与展开是同一个命题，两个方向都直接返回假设。

定理inClosed_iff声明 6
theorem inClosed_iff (a b x : ratRep) :
    InClosed a b x ↔ ratLeRep a x ∧ ratLeRep x b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 3 行
  1.   Iff.intro
    (fun (h : InClosed a b x) => h)
    (fun (h : ratLeRep a x ∧ ratLeRep x b) => h)
定理imageOn_iff声明 7
theorem imageOn_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop) (y : ratRep) :
    ImageOn f A y ↔ ∃ (x : ratRep), A x ∧ ratRel (f x) y :=
证明 · 1 步 · 3 行
  1.   Iff.intro
    (fun (h : ImageOn f A y) => h)
    (fun (h : ∃ (x : ratRep), A x ∧ ratRel (f x) y) => h)
定理isMaxOn_iff声明 8
theorem isMaxOn_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop) (M : ratRep) :
    IsMaxOn f A M ↔
      (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep (f x) M :=
证明 · 1 步 · 4 行
  1.   Iff.intro
    (fun (h : IsMaxOn f A M) => h)
    (fun (h : (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep (f x) M) => h)

从区间成员取出两端的界。单调定理一个方向用左端，另一个方向用右端。

定理inClosed_left声明 9
theorem inClosed_left (a b x : ratRep) (h : InClosed a b x) :
    ratLeRep a x :=
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   And.left (Iff.mp (inClosed_iff a b x) h)
定理inClosed_right声明 10
theorem inClosed_right (a b x : ratRep) (h : InClosed a b x) :
    ratLeRep x b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   And.right (Iff.mp (inClosed_iff a b x) h)
练习 1maxOn_upperBound定理附录解答

最大值给出像的上界

theorem maxOn_upperBound (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M : ratRep) (h : IsMaxOn f A M) :
    UpperBound ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把像里的 y 写成 f x，沿 ratRel 换成 f x ≤ y，再和最大值自带的 f x ≤ M 传递。

提示 2目标形态

UpperBound ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M。

提示 3关键件

imageOn_iff · ratRel_symm · ratLeRep_of_ratRel · ratLeRep_trans。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行

像里的元素写成 f x，先沿 ratRel 换成函数值，再用最大值自带的界。

theorem maxOn_upperBound (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M : ratRep) (h : IsMaxOn f A M) :
    UpperBound ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M :=
  fun (y : ratRep) (hy : ImageOn f A y) =>
    Exists.elim (Iff.mp (imageOn_iff f A y) hy)
      (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : A x ∧ ratRel (f x) y) =>
        ratLeRep_trans y (f x) M
          (ratLeRep_of_ratRel y (f x)
            (ratRel_symm (f x) y (And.right hx)))
          ((And.right h) x (And.left hx)))
练习 2maxOn_isSup定理附录解答

最大值就是像的上确界

theorem maxOn_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M : ratRep) (h : IsMaxOn f A M) :
    IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

上界取上一条；最小性取最大值点 c，任何上界都要压住像里的 f c，而 f c 与 M 等价。

提示 2目标形态

IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M。

提示 3关键件

maxOn_upperBound · And.left h · ratRel_symm · ratLeRep_trans。

查看解答 定理 · 14 行

上界已在上一节；最小性取最大值点 c：任何上界都要压住 f c，而 f c ~ M。

theorem maxOn_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M : ratRep) (h : IsMaxOn f A M) :
    IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M :=
  And.intro
    (maxOn_upperBound f A M h)
    (fun (C : ratRep) (hC : UpperBound ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) C) =>
      Exists.elim (And.left h)
        (fun (c : ratRep) (hc : A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) =>
          ratLeRep_trans M (f c) C
            (ratLeRep_of_ratRel M (f c)
              (ratRel_symm (f c) M (And.right hc)))
            (hC (f c)
              (Exists.intro c
                (And.intro (And.left hc) (ratRel_refl (f c)))))))

theorem isMaxOn_of_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M : ratRep) (hsup : IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M)
    (hmem : ∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) :
    IsMaxOn f A M :=

    (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : A x) =>
      (And.left hsup) (f x)
        (Exists.intro x (And.intro hx (ratRel_refl (f x)))))

练习 6maxOn_iff_isSup定理附录解答
theorem maxOn_iff_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop) :
    (∃ (M : ratRep), IsMaxOn f A M) ↔
      (∃ (M : ratRep), IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M ∧
        (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

两个方向分别是上两条定理，Iff.intro 把正、反两个蕴含接起来。

提示 2目标形态

(∃ M, IsMaxOn f A M) ↔ (∃ M, IsSup ... M ∧ ∃ c, A c ∧ f c ~ M)。

提示 3关键件

Iff.intro · exists_isSup_of_maxOn · exists_maxOn_of_isSup。

查看解答 定理 · 7 行

条件版的最值定理：最大值存在，恰好等价于像的上确界存在并且被取到。

theorem maxOn_iff_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop) :
    (∃ (M : ratRep), IsMaxOn f A M) ↔
      (∃ (M : ratRep), IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M ∧
        (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M)) :=
  Iff.intro
    (exists_isSup_of_maxOn f A)
    (exists_maxOn_of_isSup f A)
练习 7maxValue_unique定理附录解答

最大值的值唯一

theorem maxValue_unique (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M M' : ratRep) (hM : IsMaxOn f A M) (hM' : IsMaxOn f A M') :
    ratRel M M' :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

M 压住 hM' 的见证点、M' 压住 hM 的见证点，两个方向各得一条弱序，再反对称。

提示 2目标形态

IsMaxOn f A M → IsMaxOn f A M' → M ~ M'。

提示 3关键件

两次 Exists.elim · ratRel_symm · ratLeRep_trans · ratLeRep_antisymm。

查看解答 定理 · 16 行

两个最大值互相压住：M 压 M' 取 hM 的点，M' 压 M 取 hM' 的点，反对称收口。

theorem maxValue_unique (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M M' : ratRep) (hM : IsMaxOn f A M) (hM' : IsMaxOn f A M') :
    ratRel M M' :=
  Exists.elim (And.left hM)
    (fun (c : ratRep) (hc : A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) =>
      Exists.elim (And.left hM')
        (fun (d : ratRep) (hd : A d ∧ ratRel (f d) M') =>
          ratLeRep_antisymm M M'
            (ratLeRep_trans M (f c) M'
              (ratLeRep_of_ratRel M (f c)
                (ratRel_symm (f c) M (And.right hc)))
              ((And.right hM') c (And.left hc)))
            (ratLeRep_trans M' (f d) M
              (ratLeRep_of_ratRel M' (f d)
                (ratRel_symm (f d) M' (And.right hd)))
              ((And.right hM) d (And.left hd)))))
练习 8attMax_const定理附录解答

常函数：只要定义域非空就取到最大值

theorem attMax_const (A : ratRep → Prop) (c : ratRep)
    (hA : ∃ (a : ratRep), A a) :
    AttainsMaxOn (fun (_ : ratRep) => c) A :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

定义域非空给一个点 a，所有函数值都等于 c，用自反性收口。

提示 2目标形态

AttainsMaxOn (fun _ => c) A。

提示 3关键件

Exists.elim · Exists.intro · ratLeRep_refl。

查看解答 定理 · 7 行

任取定义域里的点当见证；所有函数值相等，ratLeRep_refl 直接付清。

theorem attMax_const (A : ratRep → Prop) (c : ratRep)
    (hA : ∃ (a : ratRep), A a) :
    AttainsMaxOn (fun (_ : ratRep) => c) A :=
  Exists.elim hA
    (fun (a : ratRep) (ha : A a) =>
      Exists.intro a
        (And.intro ha (fun (x : ratRep) (_hx : A x) => ratLeRep_refl c)))
练习 9attMax_of_monotone定理附录解答

单调：闭区间的右端点是最大值

theorem attMax_of_monotone (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLeRep a b) (h : NondecreasingOn f a b) :
    AttainsMaxOn f (InClosed a b) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

见证取 b；b ∈ [a,b] 用 hab 与自反，其余 x 的 x ≤ b 来自 inClosed_right，交给单调性。

提示 2目标形态

AttainsMaxOn f (InClosed a b)。

提示 3关键件

Exists.intro · And.intro · ratLeRep_refl · inClosed_right · h。

查看解答 定理 · 8 行

右端点 b 属于 [a,b] 要用 a ≤ b 与自反；其余每个 x 的界由单调性给出。

theorem attMax_of_monotone (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLeRep a b) (h : NondecreasingOn f a b) :
    AttainsMaxOn f (InClosed a b) :=
  Exists.intro b
    (And.intro (And.intro hab (ratLeRep_refl b))
      (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : InClosed a b x) =>
        h x b hx (And.intro hab (ratLeRep_refl b))
          (inClosed_right a b x hx)))
练习 10attMin_of_monotone定理附录解答
theorem attMin_of_monotone (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLeRep a b) (h : NondecreasingOn f a b) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep (f c) (f x) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

见证取 a；a ≤ x 来自 inClosed_left，单调性给出 f a ≤ f x。

提示 2目标形态

∃ c, InClosed a b c ∧ ∀ x, InClosed a b x → f c ≤ f x。

提示 3关键件

Exists.intro · ratLeRep_refl · inClosed_left · h。

查看解答 定理 · 9 行

把序反过来：左端点 a 是最小值，证明与上一条对称。

theorem attMin_of_monotone (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLeRep a b) (h : NondecreasingOn f a b) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep (f c) (f x) :=
  Exists.intro a
    (And.intro (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a) hab)
      (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : InClosed a b x) =>
        h a x (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a) hab) hx
          (inClosed_left a b x hx)))
练习 11attMax_id_closed定理附录解答

具体：闭区间上的恒等函数取到最大值

theorem attMax_id_closed :
    AttainsMaxOn (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
      (InClosed ratZeroRep ratOneRep) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

恒等函数不降（前提就是结论），0 ≤ 1 由 0 < 1 投影；交给单调定理。

提示 2目标形态

AttainsMaxOn (fun x => x) (InClosed 0 1)。

提示 3关键件

attMax_of_monotone · ratLtRep_le · ratLtRep_zero_one。

查看解答 定理 · 7 行

恒等函数在 [0,1] 上不降，交给上一条定理；0 ≤ 1 是库里的 ratLtRep_zero_one。

theorem attMax_id_closed :
    AttainsMaxOn (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
      (InClosed ratZeroRep ratOneRep) :=
  attMax_of_monotone (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep ratOneRep
    (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)
    (fun (x y : ratRep) (_hx : InClosed ratZeroRep ratOneRep x)
        (_hy : InClosed ratZeroRep ratOneRep y) (hxy : ratLeRep x y) => hxy)
练习 12ratLtRep_add_right定理附录解答

反例：开区间上的恒等函数没有最大值

theorem ratLtRep_add_right (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep x y) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

弱序用 ratLeRep_add_right；若两边相加后相等，ratRel_add_cancel_gen 消去 z 回到 x ~ y，撞上严格序的第二分量。

提示 2目标形态

x < y → x + z < y + z。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_add_right · ratLtRep_le · ratRel_add_cancel_gen · ratLtRep_right。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行

严格序对右加同一个数稳定：弱序来自 ratLeRep_add_right，严格性用加法 消去——两边相加后若相等，消去公共加数就回到原来的相等，撞上严格序。

theorem ratLtRep_add_right (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep x y) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z) :=
  And.intro
    (ratLeRep_add_right x y z (ratLtRep_le x y h))
    (fun (hxy : ratRel (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z)) =>
      ratLtRep_right x y h (ratRel_add_cancel_gen x y z hxy))
练习 13ratLtRep_self_add_pos定理附录解答
theorem ratLtRep_self_add_pos (x h : ratRep) (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ratLtRep x (ratAddRep x h) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

0 + x < h + x 由严格序加法得到；右端换成 x + h，左端用 0 + x ~ x 收回。

提示 2目标形态

0 < h → x < x + h。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_add_right · ratLtRep_congr_snd · ratAdd_comm · ratLtRep_of_le_lt · ratAdd_zero_left。

查看解答 定理 · 9 行

正数加在右边会把数抬大。0 + x < h + x 由严格序的加法得到，右端 交换成 x + h，左端再用 0 + x ~ x 收回。

theorem ratLtRep_self_add_pos (x h : ratRep) (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ratLtRep x (ratAddRep x h) :=
  ratLtRep_of_le_lt x (ratAddRep ratZeroRep x) (ratAddRep x h)
    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel x (ratAddRep ratZeroRep x)
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep ratZeroRep x) x (ratAdd_zero_left x)))
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd (ratAddRep ratZeroRep x) (ratAddRep h x)
      (ratAddRep x h)
      (ratLtRep_add_right ratZeroRep h x hh)
      (ratAdd_comm h x))
练习 14lt_add_mid定理附录解答
theorem lt_add_mid (b c : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep c b) :
    ratLtRep c (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

由 c < b 得 0 < b - c，半数仍正，再交给 ratLtRep_self_add_pos。

提示 2目标形态

c < b → c < c + (b-c)/2。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_sub_pos · ratLtRep_half · ratLtRep_self_add_pos。

查看解答 定理 · 4 行

中点严格大于左端点：0 < b - c，半数仍是正的，再交给上一条。

theorem lt_add_mid (b c : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep c b) :
    ratLtRep c (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))) :=
  ratLtRep_self_add_pos c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))
    (ratLtRep_half (ratSubRep b c) (ratLtRep_sub_pos b c h))
练习 15add_mid_lt_right定理附录解答
theorem add_mid_lt_right (b c : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep c b) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))) b :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

半数 < b-c，两边同加 c；把 (b-c)+c 收回 b，再把 h+c 交换成 c+h，左端用弱序接上交换。

提示 2目标形态

c < b → c + (b-c)/2 < b。

提示 3关键件

ratHalf_lt_self · ratLtRep_add_right · ratSubRep_add_self · ratAdd_comm · ratLeRep_of_ratRel · ratLtRep_of_le_lt。

查看解答 定理 · 16 行

中点仍落在 b 的左侧：半数小于 b - c，两边同加 c 后把 (b-c)+c 收回 b， 再把 h + c 交换成 c + h。左端用弱序接上 c + h ~ h + c。

theorem add_mid_lt_right (b c : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep c b) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))) b :=
  ratLtRep_of_le_lt
    (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)))
    (ratAddRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)) c)
    b
    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)))
      (ratAddRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)) c)
      (ratAdd_comm c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))))
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd
      (ratAddRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)) c)
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep b c) c)
      b
      (ratLtRep_add_right (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)) (ratSubRep b c) c
        (ratHalf_lt_self (ratSubRep b c) (ratLtRep_sub_pos b c h)))
      (ratSubRep_add_self b c))
练习 16not_attMax_id_open定理附录解答
theorem not_attMax_id_open :
    ¬ AttainsMaxOn (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratLtRep ratZeroRep x ∧ ratLtRep x ratOneRep) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

假设有最大值点 c，取 x = c + (1-c)/2：它比 c 大（lt_add_mid）且仍在 (0,1)（add_mid_lt_right 与传递），与 c 压住所有值矛盾。

提示 2目标形态

¬ AttainsMaxOn (fun x => x) (fun x => 0 < x ∧ x < 1)。

提示 3关键件

Exists.elim · lt_add_mid · add_mid_lt_right · ratLeRep_antisymm · ratLtRep_right。

查看解答 定理 · 29 行

任取 c ∈ (0,1)，中点 c + (1-c)/2 仍在区间里，而且严格大于 c；于是没有 哪个 c 能压住所有函数值。

theorem not_attMax_id_open :
    ¬ AttainsMaxOn (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratLtRep ratZeroRep x ∧ ratLtRep x ratOneRep) :=
  fun (h : AttainsMaxOn (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratLtRep ratZeroRep x ∧ ratLtRep x ratOneRep)) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (c : ratRep)
          (hc : (ratLtRep ratZeroRep c ∧ ratLtRep c ratOneRep) ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep),
              (ratLtRep ratZeroRep x ∧ ratLtRep x ratOneRep) →
                ratLeRep x c) =>
        ratLtRep_right c
          (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c)))
          (lt_add_mid ratOneRep c (And.right (And.left hc)))
          (ratRel_symm
            (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c))) c
            (ratLeRep_antisymm
              (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c))) c
              ((And.right hc)
                (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c)))
                (And.intro
                  (ratLtRep_trans ratZeroRep c
                    (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c)))
                    (And.left (And.left hc))
                    (lt_add_mid ratOneRep c (And.right (And.left hc))))
                  (add_mid_lt_right ratOneRep c (And.right (And.left hc)))))
              (ratLtRep_le c
                (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c)))
                (lt_add_mid ratOneRep c (And.right (And.left hc)))))))

闭区间上的最值：上确界与达到

16 道练习 · 16 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · maxOn_upperBound 定理

像里的元素写成 f x，先沿 ratRel 换成函数值，再用最大值自带的界。

theorem maxOn_upperBound (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M : ratRep) (h : IsMaxOn f A M) :
    UpperBound ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M :=
  fun (y : ratRep) (hy : ImageOn f A y) =>
    Exists.elim (Iff.mp (imageOn_iff f A y) hy)
      (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : A x ∧ ratRel (f x) y) =>
        ratLeRep_trans y (f x) M
          (ratLeRep_of_ratRel y (f x)
            (ratRel_symm (f x) y (And.right hx)))
          ((And.right h) x (And.left hx)))
练习 2 · maxOn_isSup 定理

上界已在上一节；最小性取最大值点 c：任何上界都要压住 f c，而 f c ~ M。

theorem maxOn_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M : ratRep) (h : IsMaxOn f A M) :
    IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M :=
  And.intro
    (maxOn_upperBound f A M h)
    (fun (C : ratRep) (hC : UpperBound ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) C) =>
      Exists.elim (And.left h)
        (fun (c : ratRep) (hc : A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) =>
          ratLeRep_trans M (f c) C
            (ratLeRep_of_ratRel M (f c)
              (ratRel_symm (f c) M (And.right hc)))
            (hC (f c)
              (Exists.intro c
                (And.intro (And.left hc) (ratRel_refl (f c)))))))
练习 3 · isMaxOn_of_isSup 定理

上确界的上界部分直接就是最大值的界；被取到那一半原样搬过来。

theorem isMaxOn_of_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M : ratRep) (hsup : IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M)
    (hmem : ∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) :
    IsMaxOn f A M :=
  And.intro hmem
    (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : A x) =>
      (And.left hsup) (f x)
        (Exists.intro x (And.intro hx (ratRel_refl (f x)))))
练习 4 · exists_isSup_of_maxOn 定理

最大值存在 ⇒ 像的上确界存在且被取到。

theorem exists_isSup_of_maxOn (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (h : ∃ (M : ratRep), IsMaxOn f A M) :
    ∃ (M : ratRep), IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M ∧
      (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (M : ratRep) (hM : IsMaxOn f A M) =>
      Exists.intro M
        (And.intro (maxOn_isSup f A M hM) (And.left hM)))
练习 5 · exists_maxOn_of_isSup 定理

像的上确界存在且被取到 ⇒ 最大值存在。

theorem exists_maxOn_of_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (h : ∃ (M : ratRep), IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M ∧
      (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M)) :
    ∃ (M : ratRep), IsMaxOn f A M :=
  Exists.elim h
    (fun (M : ratRep)
        (hM : IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M ∧
          (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M)) =>
      Exists.intro M
        (isMaxOn_of_isSup f A M (And.left hM) (And.right hM)))
练习 6 · maxOn_iff_isSup 定理

条件版的最值定理：最大值存在，恰好等价于像的上确界存在并且被取到。

theorem maxOn_iff_isSup (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop) :
    (∃ (M : ratRep), IsMaxOn f A M) ↔
      (∃ (M : ratRep), IsSup ratRep ratLeRep (ImageOn f A) M ∧
        (∃ (c : ratRep), A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M)) :=
  Iff.intro
    (exists_isSup_of_maxOn f A)
    (exists_maxOn_of_isSup f A)
练习 7 · maxValue_unique 定理

两个最大值互相压住：M 压 M' 取 hM 的点，M' 压 M 取 hM' 的点，反对称收口。

theorem maxValue_unique (f : ratRep → ratRep) (A : ratRep → Prop)
    (M M' : ratRep) (hM : IsMaxOn f A M) (hM' : IsMaxOn f A M') :
    ratRel M M' :=
  Exists.elim (And.left hM)
    (fun (c : ratRep) (hc : A c ∧ ratRel (f c) M) =>
      Exists.elim (And.left hM')
        (fun (d : ratRep) (hd : A d ∧ ratRel (f d) M') =>
          ratLeRep_antisymm M M'
            (ratLeRep_trans M (f c) M'
              (ratLeRep_of_ratRel M (f c)
                (ratRel_symm (f c) M (And.right hc)))
              ((And.right hM') c (And.left hc)))
            (ratLeRep_trans M' (f d) M
              (ratLeRep_of_ratRel M' (f d)
                (ratRel_symm (f d) M' (And.right hd)))
              ((And.right hM) d (And.left hd)))))
练习 8 · attMax_const 定理

任取定义域里的点当见证；所有函数值相等，ratLeRep_refl 直接付清。

theorem attMax_const (A : ratRep → Prop) (c : ratRep)
    (hA : ∃ (a : ratRep), A a) :
    AttainsMaxOn (fun (_ : ratRep) => c) A :=
  Exists.elim hA
    (fun (a : ratRep) (ha : A a) =>
      Exists.intro a
        (And.intro ha (fun (x : ratRep) (_hx : A x) => ratLeRep_refl c)))
练习 9 · attMax_of_monotone 定理

右端点 b 属于 [a,b] 要用 a ≤ b 与自反；其余每个 x 的界由单调性给出。

theorem attMax_of_monotone (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLeRep a b) (h : NondecreasingOn f a b) :
    AttainsMaxOn f (InClosed a b) :=
  Exists.intro b
    (And.intro (And.intro hab (ratLeRep_refl b))
      (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : InClosed a b x) =>
        h x b hx (And.intro hab (ratLeRep_refl b))
          (inClosed_right a b x hx)))
练习 10 · attMin_of_monotone 定理

把序反过来：左端点 a 是最小值，证明与上一条对称。

theorem attMin_of_monotone (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hab : ratLeRep a b) (h : NondecreasingOn f a b) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep (f c) (f x) :=
  Exists.intro a
    (And.intro (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a) hab)
      (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : InClosed a b x) =>
        h a x (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a) hab) hx
          (inClosed_left a b x hx)))
练习 11 · attMax_id_closed 定理

恒等函数在 [0,1] 上不降，交给上一条定理；0 ≤ 1 是库里的 ratLtRep_zero_one。

theorem attMax_id_closed :
    AttainsMaxOn (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
      (InClosed ratZeroRep ratOneRep) :=
  attMax_of_monotone (fun (x : ratRep) => x) ratZeroRep ratOneRep
    (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)
    (fun (x y : ratRep) (_hx : InClosed ratZeroRep ratOneRep x)
        (_hy : InClosed ratZeroRep ratOneRep y) (hxy : ratLeRep x y) => hxy)
练习 12 · ratLtRep_add_right 定理

严格序对右加同一个数稳定：弱序来自 ratLeRep_add_right，严格性用加法 消去——两边相加后若相等，消去公共加数就回到原来的相等，撞上严格序。

theorem ratLtRep_add_right (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep x y) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z) :=
  And.intro
    (ratLeRep_add_right x y z (ratLtRep_le x y h))
    (fun (hxy : ratRel (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z)) =>
      ratLtRep_right x y h (ratRel_add_cancel_gen x y z hxy))
练习 13 · ratLtRep_self_add_pos 定理

正数加在右边会把数抬大。0 + x < h + x 由严格序的加法得到，右端 交换成 x + h，左端再用 0 + x ~ x 收回。

theorem ratLtRep_self_add_pos (x h : ratRep) (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ratLtRep x (ratAddRep x h) :=
  ratLtRep_of_le_lt x (ratAddRep ratZeroRep x) (ratAddRep x h)
    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel x (ratAddRep ratZeroRep x)
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep ratZeroRep x) x (ratAdd_zero_left x)))
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd (ratAddRep ratZeroRep x) (ratAddRep h x)
      (ratAddRep x h)
      (ratLtRep_add_right ratZeroRep h x hh)
      (ratAdd_comm h x))
练习 14 · lt_add_mid 定理

中点严格大于左端点：0 < b - c，半数仍是正的，再交给上一条。

theorem lt_add_mid (b c : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep c b) :
    ratLtRep c (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))) :=
  ratLtRep_self_add_pos c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))
    (ratLtRep_half (ratSubRep b c) (ratLtRep_sub_pos b c h))
练习 15 · add_mid_lt_right 定理

中点仍落在 b 的左侧：半数小于 b - c，两边同加 c 后把 (b-c)+c 收回 b， 再把 h + c 交换成 c + h。左端用弱序接上 c + h ~ h + c。

theorem add_mid_lt_right (b c : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep c b) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))) b :=
  ratLtRep_of_le_lt
    (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)))
    (ratAddRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)) c)
    b
    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)))
      (ratAddRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)) c)
      (ratAdd_comm c (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c))))
    (ratLtRep_congr_snd
      (ratAddRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)) c)
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep b c) c)
      b
      (ratLtRep_add_right (ratHalf (ratSubRep b c)) (ratSubRep b c) c
        (ratHalf_lt_self (ratSubRep b c) (ratLtRep_sub_pos b c h)))
      (ratSubRep_add_self b c))
练习 16 · not_attMax_id_open 定理

任取 c ∈ (0,1)，中点 c + (1-c)/2 仍在区间里，而且严格大于 c；于是没有 哪个 c 能压住所有函数值。

theorem not_attMax_id_open :
    ¬ AttainsMaxOn (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratLtRep ratZeroRep x ∧ ratLtRep x ratOneRep) :=
  fun (h : AttainsMaxOn (fun (x : ratRep) => x)
      (fun (x : ratRep) => ratLtRep ratZeroRep x ∧ ratLtRep x ratOneRep)) =>
    Exists.elim h
      (fun (c : ratRep)
          (hc : (ratLtRep ratZeroRep c ∧ ratLtRep c ratOneRep) ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep),
              (ratLtRep ratZeroRep x ∧ ratLtRep x ratOneRep) →
                ratLeRep x c) =>
        ratLtRep_right c
          (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c)))
          (lt_add_mid ratOneRep c (And.right (And.left hc)))
          (ratRel_symm
            (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c))) c
            (ratLeRep_antisymm
              (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c))) c
              ((And.right hc)
                (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c)))
                (And.intro
                  (ratLtRep_trans ratZeroRep c
                    (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c)))
                    (And.left (And.left hc))
                    (lt_add_mid ratOneRep c (And.right (And.left hc))))
                  (add_mid_lt_right ratOneRep c (And.right (And.left hc)))))
              (ratLtRep_le c
                (ratAddRep c (ratHalf (ratSubRep ratOneRep c)))
                (lt_add_mid ratOneRep c (And.right (And.left hc)))))))

III.6 · 本章第 12 / 16 单元 · 全书第 135 / 171 单元

135最后一个负点：保号性把负点往右推一步

Unit 135 — The Last Negative Point: Sign Preservation Pushes It Right

2 个定义 · 13 个定理 · 13 道练习 · 39 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

连续函数在区间上取负值的点，能不能有「最后一个」？如果这个点 m 还在区间内部，答案是不能：f m < 0，保号性保证 m 附近一整段都取负值，而这段里有点落在 m 的右边，于是 m 不可能是最后一个。符号变化的边界如果存在，它因此不会停在负值上；能停的只剩零。

证明的力气全花在「往右找一点」上。保号性给的只是一个半径 delta，而区间右端 b 可能比 m + delta/2 更近。两种情形分开处理：若 m + delta/2 还没越过 b，就直接迈 delta/2；否则取 m 到 b 的中点，它到 m 的距离 (b-m)/2 小于 b-m，而 b-m 又不超过 delta/2，所以仍落在邻域里。

中点这一支要用到「减同一个数保持严格序」；剩下真正用到的分析事实只有一条：连续函数在一点附近保号。

边界：函数取 ratRep → ratRep，停在有理代表元层；「最后一个负点存在」在这里是反设，结论是它不存在。不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。

定义：最大元与严格负点集

「m 是 A 的最大元」：m 自己属于 A，且压住 A 里所有点。上确界不要求属于集合，最大元要求；介值定理需要的正是这个更强的「被取到」。

定义HasGreatest声明 1
def HasGreatest (A : ratRep → Prop) (m : ratRep) : Prop :=
  A m ∧ ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep x m

区间里函数值严格为负的点。

定义NegativePoints声明 2
def NegativePoints (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep) : ratRep → Prop :=
  fun (x : ratRep) => InClosed a b x ∧ ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep
练习 1hasGreatest_iff定理附录解答

定义的展开形式与投影

theorem hasGreatest_iff (A : ratRep → Prop) (m : ratRep) :
    HasGreatest A m ↔ A m ∧ ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep x m :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

定义就是合取，两个方向都直接返回假设。

提示 2目标形态

HasGreatest A m ↔ A m ∧ ∀ x, A x → x ≤ m。

提示 3关键件

Iff.intro。

查看解答 定理 · 5 行

区间里函数值不为正的点。介值定理的切点从这个集合的最大元里来。

theorem hasGreatest_iff (A : ratRep → Prop) (m : ratRep) :
    HasGreatest A m ↔ A m ∧ ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep x m :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : HasGreatest A m) => h)
    (fun (h : A m ∧ ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep x m) => h)

    ratLtRep (ratSubRep x z) (ratSubRep y z) :=

    ratLeRep (ratSubRep x z) (ratSubRep y z) :=
  ratLeRep_add_right x y (ratNegRep z) h
练习 9close_add_right定理附录解答

从 m 向右迈一步仍在 delta 邻域里

theorem close_add_right (m d eps : ratRep) (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d)
    (hde : ratLtRep d eps) : close (ratAddRep m d) m eps :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

落脚点取 m + d；距离 (m + d) - m 与 d 等价，绝对值见证取 d，剩下只要 d < eps。

提示 2目标形态

0 < d → d < eps → close (m + d) m eps。

提示 3关键件

Exists.intro · Or.inl · ratLtRep_sub_pos · ratAdd_sub_self。

查看解答 定理 · 12 行

这一步的落脚点是 m + d。距离 (m + d) - m 与 d 等价，d 是正的， 所以绝对值见证取 d 本身；剩下只要 d < eps。

theorem close_add_right (m d eps : ratRep) (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d)
    (hde : ratLtRep d eps) : close (ratAddRep m d) m eps :=
  Exists.intro d
    (And.intro
      (Or.inl
        (And.intro
          (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep m d) m)
            (ratLtRep_sub_pos (ratAddRep m d) m
              (ratLtRep_self_add_pos m d hd)))
          (ratRel_symm (ratSubRep (ratAddRep m d) m) d
            (ratAdd_sub_self m d))))
      hde)
练习 10half_sub_lt_delta定理附录解答
theorem half_sub_lt_delta (b m delta : ratRep) (hmb : ratLtRep m b)
    (hble : ratLeRep b (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)))
    (hdelta : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta) :
    ratLtRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)) delta :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

(b-m)/2 < b-m（半数小于自身）；b-m ≤ delta/2 由 hble 减 m 得到；再 delta/2 < delta。

提示 2目标形态

m < b → b ≤ m + delta/2 → 0 < delta → (b-m)/2 < delta。

提示 3关键件

ratHalf_lt_self · ratLtRep_sub_pos · ratLeRep_sub_right · ratAdd_sub_self · ratLtRep_of_le_lt · ratLtRep_trans。

查看解答 定理 · 14 行

若 b 还落在 m + delta/2 以内，m 到 b 的中点与 m 的距离就小于 delta： 这个距离是 (b-m)/2，小于 b-m，而 b-m ≤ delta/2 < delta。

theorem half_sub_lt_delta (b m delta : ratRep) (hmb : ratLtRep m b)
    (hble : ratLeRep b (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)))
    (hdelta : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta) :
    ratLtRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)) delta :=
  ratLtRep_trans (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)) (ratSubRep b m) delta
    (ratHalf_lt_self (ratSubRep b m) (ratLtRep_sub_pos b m hmb))
    (ratLtRep_of_le_lt (ratSubRep b m) (ratHalf delta) delta
      (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep b m)
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) m) (ratHalf delta)
        (ratLeRep_sub_right b (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) m hble)
        (ratLeRep_of_ratRel
          (ratSubRep (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) m) (ratHalf delta)
          (ratAdd_sub_self m (ratHalf delta))))
      (ratHalf_lt_self delta hdelta))
练习 11exists_negative_right定理附录解答

保号性把负点往右推一步

theorem exists_negative_right (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hmem : InClosed a b m) (hmb : ratLtRep m b)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f m) (hneg : ratLtRep (f m) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (y : ratRep), InClosed a b y ∧ ratLtRep m y ∧
      ratLtRep (f y) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先用 negativeNear_of_continuousAt 取保号半径 delta；分 m + delta/2 ≤ b 与 b ≤ m + delta/2 两支，各自用 close_add_right 与中点构造落脚点。

提示 2目标形态

∃ y, y ∈ [a,b] ∧ m < y ∧ f y < 0。

提示 3关键件

negativeNear_of_continuousAt · ratLeRep_total · close_add_right · lt_add_mid · add_mid_lt_right · half_sub_lt_delta。

查看解答 定理 · 50 行
theorem exists_negative_right (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hmem : InClosed a b m) (hmb : ratLtRep m b)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f m) (hneg : ratLtRep (f m) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (y : ratRep), InClosed a b y ∧ ratLtRep m y ∧
      ratLtRep (f y) ratZeroRep :=
  Exists.elim (negativeNear_of_continuousAt f m hf hneg)
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x m delta →
            ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep) =>
      Or.elim
        (fun (hle : ratLeRep (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) b) =>
          Exists.intro (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))
            (And.intro
              (And.intro
                (ratLeRep_trans a m (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))
                  (inClosed_left a b m hmem)
                  (ratLtRep_le m (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))
                    (ratLtRep_self_add_pos m (ratHalf delta)
                      (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd)))))
                hle)
              (And.intro
                (ratLtRep_self_add_pos m (ratHalf delta)
                  (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd)))
                ((fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x m delta) =>
                    (And.right hd) x hx)
                  (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))
                  (close_add_right m (ratHalf delta) delta
                    (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))
                    (ratHalf_lt_self delta (And.left hd)))))))
        (fun (hble : ratLeRep b (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))) =>
          Exists.intro (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)))
            (And.intro
              (And.intro
                (ratLeRep_trans a m (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)))
                  (inClosed_left a b m hmem)
                  (ratLtRep_le m (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)))
                    (lt_add_mid b m hmb)))
                (ratLtRep_le (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m))) b
                  (add_mid_lt_right b m hmb)))
              (And.intro
                (lt_add_mid b m hmb)
                ((fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x m delta) =>
                    (And.right hd) x hx)
                  (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)))
                  (close_add_right m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)) delta
                    (ratLtRep_half (ratSubRep b m)
                      (ratLtRep_sub_pos b m hmb))
                    (half_sub_lt_delta b m delta hmb hble (And.left hd)))))))
        (ratLeRep_total (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) b))
练习 12no_greatest_negative_at定理附录解答

严格负点集没有最大元

theorem no_greatest_negative_at (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m)
    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) (hf : ContinuousAt f m) : False :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

上一条给出更靠右的严格负点 y；它属于集合，最大性给 y ≤ m，与 m < y 反对称冲突。

提示 2目标形态

HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m → m < b → ContinuousAt f m → False。

提示 3关键件

exists_negative_right · hasGreatest_upper · negativePoints_mem/value · ratLeRep_antisymm · ratLtRep_right。

查看解答 定理 · 22 行

若 m 是严格负点集的最大元且 m 在区间内部，上一条给出一个更靠右的严格 负点 y；它属于集合，于是 y ≤ m，与 m < y 冲突。

theorem no_greatest_negative_at (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m)
    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) (hf : ContinuousAt f m) : False :=
  @Exists.elim ratRep
    (fun (y : ratRep) => InClosed a b y ∧ ratLtRep m y ∧
      ratLtRep (f y) ratZeroRep)
    False
    (exists_negative_right f a b m
      (negativePoints_mem f a b m
        (hasGreatest_mem (NegativePoints f a b) m hgreat))
      hmb hf
      (negativePoints_value f a b m
        (hasGreatest_mem (NegativePoints f a b) m hgreat)))
    (fun (y : ratRep)
        (hy : InClosed a b y ∧ ratLtRep m y ∧
          ratLtRep (f y) ratZeroRep) =>
      ratLtRep_right m y (And.left (And.right hy))
        (ratRel_symm y m
          (ratLeRep_antisymm y m
            (hasGreatest_upper (NegativePoints f a b) m hgreat y
              (And.intro (And.left hy) (And.right (And.right hy))))
            (ratLtRep_le m y (And.left (And.right hy))))))
练习 13no_last_negative定理附录解答

打包：严格负点集根本没有最大元

theorem no_last_negative (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hcont : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ContinuousAt f x) :
    ¬ (∃ (m : ratRep), HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧
      ratLtRep m b) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

反设取出 m 与它的最大性、以及 m < b；连续性由 hcont m 配 m 的区间成员得到，再交给上一条。

提示 2目标形态

¬ ∃ m, HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧ m < b。

提示 3关键件

Exists.elim · no_greatest_negative_at · negativePoints_mem · hasGreatest_mem。

查看解答 定理 · 16 行

上一条是「给定 m，若 m 是最大元则矛盾」；把它写成对 m 的否定，就是 这个单元要说的事：在区间内部，严格负点集不会有最后一个点。

theorem no_last_negative (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hcont : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ContinuousAt f x) :
    ¬ (∃ (m : ratRep), HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧
      ratLtRep m b) :=
  fun (h : ∃ (m : ratRep), HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧
      ratLtRep m b) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (m : ratRep) => HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧
        ratLtRep m b)
      False h
      (fun (m : ratRep)
          (hm : HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧ ratLtRep m b) =>
        no_greatest_negative_at f a b m (And.left hm) (And.right hm)
          (hcont m
            (negativePoints_mem f a b m
              (hasGreatest_mem (NegativePoints f a b) m (And.left hm)))))

最后一个负点：保号性把负点往右推一步

13 道练习 · 13 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · hasGreatest_iff 定理

区间里函数值不为正的点。介值定理的切点从这个集合的最大元里来。

theorem hasGreatest_iff (A : ratRep → Prop) (m : ratRep) :
    HasGreatest A m ↔ A m ∧ ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep x m :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : HasGreatest A m) => h)
    (fun (h : A m ∧ ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep x m) => h)
练习 2 · hasGreatest_mem 定理
theorem hasGreatest_mem (A : ratRep → Prop) (m : ratRep)
    (h : HasGreatest A m) : A m :=
  And.left (Iff.mp (hasGreatest_iff A m) h)
练习 3 · hasGreatest_upper 定理
theorem hasGreatest_upper (A : ratRep → Prop) (m : ratRep)
    (h : HasGreatest A m) : ∀ (x : ratRep), A x → ratLeRep x m :=
  And.right (Iff.mp (hasGreatest_iff A m) h)
练习 4 · negativePoints_iff 定理
theorem negativePoints_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b x : ratRep) :
    NegativePoints f a b x ↔ InClosed a b x ∧ ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : NegativePoints f a b x) => h)
    (fun (h : InClosed a b x ∧ ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep) => h)
练习 5 · negativePoints_mem 定理

点集的投影单独取名：HasGreatest 的见证是定义别名，直接在它上面用 And.left / And.right 时，隐式参数补不出来；先展开成合取再投影。

theorem negativePoints_mem (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b x : ratRep)
    (h : NegativePoints f a b x) : InClosed a b x :=
  And.left (Iff.mp (negativePoints_iff f a b x) h)
练习 6 · negativePoints_value 定理
theorem negativePoints_value (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b x : ratRep)
    (h : NegativePoints f a b x) : ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  And.right (Iff.mp (negativePoints_iff f a b x) h)
练习 7 · ratLtRep_sub_right 定理
theorem ratLtRep_sub_right (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep x y) :
    ratLtRep (ratSubRep x z) (ratSubRep y z) :=
  ratLtRep_add_right x y (ratNegRep z) h
练习 8 · ratLeRep_sub_right 定理

同一个换形对弱序也成立，第二条分支只要 b ≤ m + delta/2 就够用。

theorem ratLeRep_sub_right (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLeRep x y) :
    ratLeRep (ratSubRep x z) (ratSubRep y z) :=
  ratLeRep_add_right x y (ratNegRep z) h
练习 9 · close_add_right 定理

这一步的落脚点是 m + d。距离 (m + d) - m 与 d 等价，d 是正的， 所以绝对值见证取 d 本身；剩下只要 d < eps。

theorem close_add_right (m d eps : ratRep) (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep d)
    (hde : ratLtRep d eps) : close (ratAddRep m d) m eps :=
  Exists.intro d
    (And.intro
      (Or.inl
        (And.intro
          (ratLtRep_le ratZeroRep (ratSubRep (ratAddRep m d) m)
            (ratLtRep_sub_pos (ratAddRep m d) m
              (ratLtRep_self_add_pos m d hd)))
          (ratRel_symm (ratSubRep (ratAddRep m d) m) d
            (ratAdd_sub_self m d))))
      hde)
练习 10 · half_sub_lt_delta 定理

若 b 还落在 m + delta/2 以内，m 到 b 的中点与 m 的距离就小于 delta： 这个距离是 (b-m)/2，小于 b-m，而 b-m ≤ delta/2 < delta。

theorem half_sub_lt_delta (b m delta : ratRep) (hmb : ratLtRep m b)
    (hble : ratLeRep b (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)))
    (hdelta : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta) :
    ratLtRep (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)) delta :=
  ratLtRep_trans (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)) (ratSubRep b m) delta
    (ratHalf_lt_self (ratSubRep b m) (ratLtRep_sub_pos b m hmb))
    (ratLtRep_of_le_lt (ratSubRep b m) (ratHalf delta) delta
      (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep b m)
        (ratSubRep (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) m) (ratHalf delta)
        (ratLeRep_sub_right b (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) m hble)
        (ratLeRep_of_ratRel
          (ratSubRep (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) m) (ratHalf delta)
          (ratAdd_sub_self m (ratHalf delta))))
      (ratHalf_lt_self delta hdelta))
练习 11 · exists_negative_right 定理
theorem exists_negative_right (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hmem : InClosed a b m) (hmb : ratLtRep m b)
    (hf : ContinuousAt f m) (hneg : ratLtRep (f m) ratZeroRep) :
    ∃ (y : ratRep), InClosed a b y ∧ ratLtRep m y ∧
      ratLtRep (f y) ratZeroRep :=
  Exists.elim (negativeNear_of_continuousAt f m hf hneg)
    (fun (delta : ratRep)
        (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), close x m delta →
            ratLtRep (f x) ratZeroRep) =>
      Or.elim
        (fun (hle : ratLeRep (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) b) =>
          Exists.intro (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))
            (And.intro
              (And.intro
                (ratLeRep_trans a m (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))
                  (inClosed_left a b m hmem)
                  (ratLtRep_le m (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))
                    (ratLtRep_self_add_pos m (ratHalf delta)
                      (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd)))))
                hle)
              (And.intro
                (ratLtRep_self_add_pos m (ratHalf delta)
                  (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd)))
                ((fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x m delta) =>
                    (And.right hd) x hx)
                  (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))
                  (close_add_right m (ratHalf delta) delta
                    (ratLtRep_half delta (And.left hd))
                    (ratHalf_lt_self delta (And.left hd)))))))
        (fun (hble : ratLeRep b (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta))) =>
          Exists.intro (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)))
            (And.intro
              (And.intro
                (ratLeRep_trans a m (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)))
                  (inClosed_left a b m hmem)
                  (ratLtRep_le m (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)))
                    (lt_add_mid b m hmb)))
                (ratLtRep_le (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m))) b
                  (add_mid_lt_right b m hmb)))
              (And.intro
                (lt_add_mid b m hmb)
                ((fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x m delta) =>
                    (And.right hd) x hx)
                  (ratAddRep m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)))
                  (close_add_right m (ratHalf (ratSubRep b m)) delta
                    (ratLtRep_half (ratSubRep b m)
                      (ratLtRep_sub_pos b m hmb))
                    (half_sub_lt_delta b m delta hmb hble (And.left hd)))))))
        (ratLeRep_total (ratAddRep m (ratHalf delta)) b))
练习 12 · no_greatest_negative_at 定理

若 m 是严格负点集的最大元且 m 在区间内部，上一条给出一个更靠右的严格 负点 y；它属于集合，于是 y ≤ m，与 m < y 冲突。

theorem no_greatest_negative_at (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m)
    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) (hf : ContinuousAt f m) : False :=
  @Exists.elim ratRep
    (fun (y : ratRep) => InClosed a b y ∧ ratLtRep m y ∧
      ratLtRep (f y) ratZeroRep)
    False
    (exists_negative_right f a b m
      (negativePoints_mem f a b m
        (hasGreatest_mem (NegativePoints f a b) m hgreat))
      hmb hf
      (negativePoints_value f a b m
        (hasGreatest_mem (NegativePoints f a b) m hgreat)))
    (fun (y : ratRep)
        (hy : InClosed a b y ∧ ratLtRep m y ∧
          ratLtRep (f y) ratZeroRep) =>
      ratLtRep_right m y (And.left (And.right hy))
        (ratRel_symm y m
          (ratLeRep_antisymm y m
            (hasGreatest_upper (NegativePoints f a b) m hgreat y
              (And.intro (And.left hy) (And.right (And.right hy))))
            (ratLtRep_le m y (And.left (And.right hy))))))
练习 13 · no_last_negative 定理

上一条是「给定 m，若 m 是最大元则矛盾」；把它写成对 m 的否定，就是 这个单元要说的事：在区间内部，严格负点集不会有最后一个点。

theorem no_last_negative (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hcont : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ContinuousAt f x) :
    ¬ (∃ (m : ratRep), HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧
      ratLtRep m b) :=
  fun (h : ∃ (m : ratRep), HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧
      ratLtRep m b) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (m : ratRep) => HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧
        ratLtRep m b)
      False h
      (fun (m : ratRep)
          (hm : HasGreatest (NegativePoints f a b) m ∧ ratLtRep m b) =>
        no_greatest_negative_at f a b m (And.left hm) (And.right hm)
          (hcont m
            (negativePoints_mem f a b m
              (hasGreatest_mem (NegativePoints f a b) m (And.left hm)))))

III.6 · 本章第 13 / 16 单元 · 全书第 136 / 171 单元

136介值定理：最后一个非正点

Unit 136 — The Intermediate Value Theorem: The Last Nonpositive Point

2 个定义 · 11 个定理 · 11 道练习 · 33 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

连续函数从一个负值走到一个正值，中间必有一点取零。在 ℚ 上这句话不能无条件成立：x ↦ x² - 2 在 [1,2] 上连续，两端异号，却没有任何有理点使它取零。二分法把区间越切越短，切到的是一个实数，而 ℚ 里没有那个点。所以缺的不是连续性，而是「切点存在」。

把缺的那一块写成假设，剩下的全是连续性的工作。设 m 是区间里所有非正点的最大元，且 m 严格在右端点左边。上一单元证明了严格负点集没有最大元，所以 f m 不能小于零；而 m 属于非正点集，f m 又不能大于零。两头一夹，f m 只能等于零。

最后把结论换成介值的语言：f a < 0 < f b，非正点集有最大元 m，那么 m 落在区间内部，且 f m ~ 0。为了让「m 在内部」这句话有内容，函数还得尊重代表元的等价：等价的两个点给出等价的函数值。没有这一条，同一个实数的两个代表元可以取到不同的值，端点附近的论证就断了。

边界：函数取 ratRep → ratRep，停在有理代表元层；「非正点集有最大元」是完整介值定理在 ℚ 上真正缺的那一步，这里把它作为显式假设，不写成公理。不碰 kernel/front。

定义：非正点集与「尊重等价」

区间里函数值不为正的点。介值定理的切点从这个集合的最大元里来。

定义NonpositivePoints声明 1
def NonpositivePoints (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep) : ratRep → Prop :=
  fun (x : ratRep) => InClosed a b x ∧ ratLeRep (f x) ratZeroRep

函数尊重代表元的等价：等价的两个输入给出等价的输出。这是「函数定义在实数上」而不是「定义在代表元上」的形式说法。

定义RespectsRel声明 2
def RespectsRel (f : ratRep → ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x y : ratRep), ratRel x y → ratRel (f x) (f y)

端点不在切点上

theorem not_rel_left_of_negative (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f) (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hzero : ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep) : ¬ ratRel a m :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

若 a ~ m，则 f a ~ f m ~ 0；用传递性把 f a ~ 0 交给 f a < 0 的第二分量。

提示 2目标形态

f a < 0 → f m ~ 0 → ¬ a ~ m。

提示 3关键件

hwf · ratRel_trans_gen · ratLtRep_right。

查看解答 定理 · 7 行

f a < 0 而 f m 是零点：a 与 m 不可能等价，否则 f a 也会等价于零。 这条不等式要等「m 是零点」证完才用得上。

theorem not_rel_left_of_negative (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f) (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hzero : ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep) : ¬ ratRel a m :=
  fun (h : ratRel a m) =>
    ratLtRep_right (f a) ratZeroRep hfa
      (ratRel_trans_gen (f a) (f m) ratZeroRep
        (hwf a m h) hzero)
练习 8not_rel_right_of_positive定理附录解答
theorem not_rel_right_of_positive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (b m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f) (hfb : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f b))
    (hle : ratLeRep (f m) ratZeroRep) : ¬ ratRel m b :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

若 m ~ b，则 f m ~ f b；由 0 < f b 得 0 < f m，再与 f m ≤ 0 夹出 0 < 0。

提示 2目标形态

0 < f b → f m ≤ 0 → ¬ m ~ b。

提示 3关键件

hwf · ratLtRep_congr_snd · ratLtRep_of_lt_le · ratLtRep_right · ratRel_refl。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行

f b > 0 而 f m ≤ 0：m 与 b 不可能等价。否则 f m ~ f b > 0，撞上 f m ≤ 0。这一条只用到非正，不依赖零点，所以能先用来断言 m 不是右端点。

theorem not_rel_right_of_positive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (b m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f) (hfb : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f b))
    (hle : ratLeRep (f m) ratZeroRep) : ¬ ratRel m b :=
  fun (h : ratRel m b) =>
    ratLtRep_right ratZeroRep ratZeroRep
      (ratLtRep_of_lt_le ratZeroRep (f m) ratZeroRep
        (ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep (f b) (f m) hfb
          (ratRel_symm (f m) (f b) (hwf m b h)))
        hle)
      (ratRel_refl ratZeroRep)

非正点集的最大元是零点

theorem zero_of_greatest_nonpositive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)

    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) (hf : ContinuousAt f m) :
    ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep :=

提示 1思路

f m ≤ 0 分「等于零」与「严格小于零」两支。后一支把 m 看成严格负点集的最大元（严格负都非正），交给库里的 no_greatest_negative_at。

提示 2目标形态

HasGreatest 非正点集 m → m < b → ContinuousAt f m → f m ~ 0。

查看解答 定理 · 19 行
theorem zero_of_greatest_nonpositive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m)
    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) (hf : ContinuousAt f m) :
    ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (heq : ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep) => heq)
    (fun (hlt : ratLtRep (f m) ratZeroRep) =>
      False.elim
        (no_greatest_negative_at f a b m
          (And.intro
            (And.intro
              (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat)
              hlt)
            (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : NegativePoints f a b x) =>
              hasGreatest_upper (NonpositivePoints f a b) m hgreat x
                (nonpositive_of_negative f a b x hx)))
          hmb hf))
    (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt (f m) ratZeroRep
      (hasGreatest_nonpositive_value f a b m hgreat))
练习 10interior_of_zero定理附录解答

介值定理：零点落在区间内部

theorem interior_of_zero (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f)
    (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m)
    (hzero : ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep)
    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧ ratLtRep a c ∧ ratLtRep c b ∧
      ratRel (f c) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

见证取 m；区间成员来自最大元，a < m 由 not_rel_left_of_negative 排除左端点，m < b 直接是前提。

提示 2目标形态

∃ c, c ∈ [a,b] ∧ a < c ∧ c < b ∧ f c ~ 0。

提示 3关键件

Exists.intro · hasGreatest_upper · not_rel_left_of_negative · nonpositive_of_negative。

查看解答 定理 · 25 行
theorem interior_of_zero (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f)
    (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m)
    (hzero : ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep)
    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧ ratLtRep a c ∧ ratLtRep c b ∧
      ratRel (f c) ratZeroRep :=
  Exists.intro m
    (And.intro
      (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat)
      (And.intro
        (And.intro
          (hasGreatest_upper (NonpositivePoints f a b) m hgreat a
            (And.intro
              (And.intro
                (ratLeRep_refl a)
                (ratLeRep_trans a m b
                  (inClosed_left a b m
                    (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat))
                  (inClosed_right a b m
                    (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat))))
              (ratLtRep_le (f a) ratZeroRep hfa)))
          (not_rel_left_of_negative f a m hwf hfa hzero))
        (And.intro hmb hzero)))
练习 11ivt_of_greatest_nonpositive定理附录解答
theorem ivt_of_greatest_nonpositive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hcont : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ContinuousAt f x)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f)
    (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hfb : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f b))
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧ ratLtRep a c ∧ ratLtRep c b ∧
      ratRel (f c) ratZeroRep :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先由 not_rel_right_of_positive 排除 m ~ b，得到 m < b；再用 zero_of_greatest_nonpositive 得零点；最后交给 interior_of_zero。

提示 2目标形态

连续 + f a < 0 < f b + 最大元 → ∃ 内部零点。

提示 3关键件

not_rel_right_of_positive · zero_of_greatest_nonpositive · interior_of_zero · hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem/value。

查看解答 定理 · 21 行

介值定理的条件版：f 在 [a,b] 上连续、f a < 0 < f b、非正点集有最大元 m，并且函数尊重代表元的等价。结论是区间内部有一个零点。

theorem ivt_of_greatest_nonpositive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hcont : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ContinuousAt f x)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f)
    (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hfb : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f b))
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧ ratLtRep a c ∧ ratLtRep c b ∧
      ratRel (f c) ratZeroRep :=
  interior_of_zero f a b m hwf hfa hgreat
    (zero_of_greatest_nonpositive f a b m hgreat
      (And.intro
        (inClosed_right a b m
          (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat))
        (not_rel_right_of_positive f b m hwf hfb
          (hasGreatest_nonpositive_value f a b m hgreat)))
      (hcont m (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat)))
    (And.intro
      (inClosed_right a b m
        (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat))
      (not_rel_right_of_positive f b m hwf hfb
        (hasGreatest_nonpositive_value f a b m hgreat)))

介值定理：最后一个非正点

11 道练习 · 11 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · nonpositivePoints_iff 定理

区间里函数值严格为负的点。

theorem nonpositivePoints_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b x : ratRep) :
    NonpositivePoints f a b x ↔ InClosed a b x ∧ ratLeRep (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : NonpositivePoints f a b x) => h)
    (fun (h : InClosed a b x ∧ ratLeRep (f x) ratZeroRep) => h)
练习 2 · nonpositive_of_negative 定理

严格负的数一定非正：介值定理的两个点集之间就差这一条投影。 严格负的数一定非正：两个点集之间就差这一条投影。

theorem nonpositive_of_negative (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b x : ratRep)
    (h : NegativePoints f a b x) : NonpositivePoints f a b x :=
  And.intro (And.left h)
    (ratLtRep_le (f x) ratZeroRep (And.right h))
练习 3 · nonpositivePoints_mem 定理

两个点集的投影单独取名：HasGreatest 的见证是定义别名，直接在它上面 用 And.left / And.right 时，隐式参数补不出来；先展开成合取再投影。 在 HasGreatest 的见证上直接取 And.left / And.right 补不出隐式参数， 所以先展开成合取，再投影。

theorem nonpositivePoints_mem (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b x : ratRep)
    (h : NonpositivePoints f a b x) : InClosed a b x :=
  And.left (Iff.mp (nonpositivePoints_iff f a b x) h)
练习 4 · nonpositivePoints_value 定理
theorem nonpositivePoints_value (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b x : ratRep)
    (h : NonpositivePoints f a b x) : ratLeRep (f x) ratZeroRep :=
  And.right (Iff.mp (nonpositivePoints_iff f a b x) h)
练习 5 · hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem 定理
theorem hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (h : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m) : InClosed a b m :=
  nonpositivePoints_mem f a b m
    (hasGreatest_mem (NonpositivePoints f a b) m h)
练习 6 · hasGreatest_nonpositive_value 定理
theorem hasGreatest_nonpositive_value (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (h : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m) :
    ratLeRep (f m) ratZeroRep :=
  nonpositivePoints_value f a b m
    (hasGreatest_mem (NonpositivePoints f a b) m h)
练习 7 · not_rel_left_of_negative 定理

f a < 0 而 f m 是零点：a 与 m 不可能等价，否则 f a 也会等价于零。 这条不等式要等「m 是零点」证完才用得上。

theorem not_rel_left_of_negative (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f) (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hzero : ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep) : ¬ ratRel a m :=
  fun (h : ratRel a m) =>
    ratLtRep_right (f a) ratZeroRep hfa
      (ratRel_trans_gen (f a) (f m) ratZeroRep
        (hwf a m h) hzero)
练习 8 · not_rel_right_of_positive 定理

f b > 0 而 f m ≤ 0：m 与 b 不可能等价。否则 f m ~ f b > 0，撞上 f m ≤ 0。这一条只用到非正，不依赖零点，所以能先用来断言 m 不是右端点。

theorem not_rel_right_of_positive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (b m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f) (hfb : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f b))
    (hle : ratLeRep (f m) ratZeroRep) : ¬ ratRel m b :=
  fun (h : ratRel m b) =>
    ratLtRep_right ratZeroRep ratZeroRep
      (ratLtRep_of_lt_le ratZeroRep (f m) ratZeroRep
        (ratLtRep_congr_snd ratZeroRep (f b) (f m) hfb
          (ratRel_symm (f m) (f b) (hwf m b h)))
        hle)
      (ratRel_refl ratZeroRep)
练习 9 · zero_of_greatest_nonpositive 定理
theorem zero_of_greatest_nonpositive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m)
    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) (hf : ContinuousAt f m) :
    ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (heq : ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep) => heq)
    (fun (hlt : ratLtRep (f m) ratZeroRep) =>
      False.elim
        (no_greatest_negative_at f a b m
          (And.intro
            (And.intro
              (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat)
              hlt)
            (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : NegativePoints f a b x) =>
              hasGreatest_upper (NonpositivePoints f a b) m hgreat x
                (nonpositive_of_negative f a b x hx)))
          hmb hf))
    (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt (f m) ratZeroRep
      (hasGreatest_nonpositive_value f a b m hgreat))
练习 10 · interior_of_zero 定理
theorem interior_of_zero (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f)
    (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m)
    (hzero : ratRel (f m) ratZeroRep)
    (hmb : ratLtRep m b) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧ ratLtRep a c ∧ ratLtRep c b ∧
      ratRel (f c) ratZeroRep :=
  Exists.intro m
    (And.intro
      (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat)
      (And.intro
        (And.intro
          (hasGreatest_upper (NonpositivePoints f a b) m hgreat a

              (And.intro
                (ratLeRep_refl a)
                (ratLeRep_trans a m b
                  (inClosed_left a b m
                    (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat))
                  (inClosed_right a b m
                    (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat))))
              (ratLtRep_le (f a) ratZeroRep hfa)))
          (not_rel_left_of_negative f a m hwf hfa hzero))
        (And.intro hmb hzero)))
练习 11 · ivt_of_greatest_nonpositive 定理

介值定理的条件版：f 在 [a,b] 上连续、f a < 0 < f b、非正点集有最大元 m，并且函数尊重代表元的等价。结论是区间内部有一个零点。

theorem ivt_of_greatest_nonpositive (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b m : ratRep)
    (hcont : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ContinuousAt f x)
    (hwf : RespectsRel f)
    (hfa : ratLtRep (f a) ratZeroRep)
    (hfb : ratLtRep ratZeroRep (f b))
    (hgreat : HasGreatest (NonpositivePoints f a b) m) :
    ∃ (c : ratRep), InClosed a b c ∧ ratLtRep a c ∧ ratLtRep c b ∧
      ratRel (f c) ratZeroRep :=

    (zero_of_greatest_nonpositive f a b m hgreat
