        (inClosed_right a b m
          (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat))
        (not_rel_right_of_positive f b m hwf hfb
          (hasGreatest_nonpositive_value f a b m hgreat)))
      (hcont m (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat)))
    (And.intro
      (inClosed_right a b m
        (hasGreatest_nonpositive_mem f a b m hgreat))
      (not_rel_right_of_positive f b m hwf hfb
        (hasGreatest_nonpositive_value f a b m hgreat)))

III.6 · 本章第 14 / 16 单元 · 全书第 137 / 171 单元

137无穷远处的极限：越过分界线之后靠近 L

Unit 137 — Limits at Infinity: Getting Close Beyond a Threshold

1 个定义 · 7 个定理 · 7 道练习 · 21 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

序列的极限只问下标越来越大时项往哪里去；函数没有下标，只有自变量。若让自变量一直往右走，函数值会不会也停在某个数附近？这正是「无穷远处的极限」要回答的问题。它把 ε-N 里的阈值 N 换成一条有理数的分界线 M：只要 x 在 M 的右边，f x 就落在 L 的 ε 邻域里。

与一点处的极限相比，这里没有「挖掉 a」的必要，因为 x 只从右边来，而且没有一个特殊的点需要排除。δ 变成了朝一个方向的 M，量词顺序不变：先给 ε，再找 M，最后对每个越过 M 的 x 给结论。

常函数在任何 M 处都合格；把图像左右平移，极限不变；两个函数从某条分界线起逐点相等，极限也相同。恒等函数则给出反例：它一直往右走，不可能停在某个有理数 L 附近，所以「在无穷远处有有限极限」不是自动的。

边界：函数取 ratRep → ratRep，极限停在有理代表元层；不把 ±∞ 当成数，也不讨论函数值趋于无穷。不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。

定义：越过某条线之后一直靠近 L

阈值 M 是有理数，不要求为正：它只是数轴上的一条分界线。ratLeRep M x 读作「x 在 M 的右边或与 M 重合」，于是量词只朝一个方向走。

定义TendsToAtInfinity声明 1
def TendsToAtInfinity (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
    ∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close (f x) L eps
练习 1tendsToAtInfinity_iff定理附录解答

定义的展开形式

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep) :
    TendsToAtInfinity f L ↔
      ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
        ∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close (f x) L eps :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 TendsToAtInfinity 展开，右边那串量词逐字就是定义；两个方向都直接返回假设。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity f L ↔ ∀ eps > 0, ∃ M, ∀ x, M ≤ x → close (f x) L eps。

提示 3关键件

Iff.intro · 定义性展开。

查看解答 定理 · 9 行

定义与展开是同一个命题，两个方向都直接返回假设。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep) :
    TendsToAtInfinity f L ↔
      ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
        ∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close (f x) L eps :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : TendsToAtInfinity f L) => h)
    (fun (h : ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
        ∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
          close (f x) L eps) => h)
练习 2exists_ge_both定理附录解答

两个阈值可以合成一个

和律要把两条分界线并成一条。全性只给「a ≤ b 或 b ≤ a」两支；两支各自挑较大的那个当 M，另一条弱序由传递性补上。

theorem exists_ge_both (a b : ratRep) :
    ∃ (M : ratRep), ratLeRep a M ∧ ratLeRep b M :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 ratLeRep_total a b 分两支；第一支取 b，第二支取 a，再用自反性补齐另一边。

提示 2目标形态

∃ M, ratLeRep a M ∧ ratLeRep b M。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_total · ratLeRep_refl · Exists.intro · And.intro。

查看解答 定理 · 8 行

全性只给「a ≤ b 或 b ≤ a」两支；两支各自挑较大的那个当 M，另一条 弱序由传递性补上。

theorem exists_ge_both (a b : ratRep) :
    ∃ (M : ratRep), ratLeRep a M ∧ ratLeRep b M :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (hab : ratLeRep a b) =>
      Exists.intro b (And.intro hab (ratLeRep_refl b)))
    (fun (hba : ratLeRep b a) =>
      Exists.intro a (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a) hba))
    (ratLeRep_total a b)
练习 3tendsToAtInfinity_const定理附录解答

常函数

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_const (c : ratRep) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (_ : ratRep) => c) c :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

常函数的输出与 L 相等，相等就落在每个正 ε 内；分界线取零即可。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity (fun _ => c) c。

提示 3关键件

ratZeroRep · close_of_ratRel · ratRel_refl。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行

常函数的输出与 L 相等，相等就落在每个正 ε 内；分界线取零即可。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_const (c : ratRep) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (_ : ratRep) => c) c :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    Exists.intro ratZeroRep
      (fun (x : ratRep) (_hx : ratLeRep ratZeroRep x) =>
        close_of_ratRel c c (ratRel_refl c) eps heps)
练习 4tendsToAtInfinity_shift定理附录解答

平移不改变无穷远处的极限

若 f 在 M 之后靠近 L，那么 x + c 在 M - c 之后靠近 L。分界线往前挪一个 c：x ≥ M - c 两边加 c，左端用 (M - c) + c ~ M 收回。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_shift (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep)
    (h : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => f (ratAddRep x c)) L :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

给定 ε，向 f 的极限要 M；新分界线取 M - c，两边加 c 后用 (M - c) + c ~ M 搬回。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity (fun x => f (x + c)) L。

提示 3关键件

ratSubRep_add_self · ratLeRep_add_right · ratLeRep_congr_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 16 行
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_shift (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep)
    (h : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => f (ratAddRep x c)) L :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    Exists.elim (h eps heps)
      (fun (M : ratRep)
          (hM : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close (f x) L eps) =>
        Exists.intro (ratSubRep M c)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLeRep (ratSubRep M c) x) =>
            hM (ratAddRep x c)
              (ratLeRep_congr_gen
                (ratAddRep (ratSubRep M c) c) M
                (ratAddRep x c) (ratAddRep x c)
                (ratSubRep_add_self M c)
                (ratRel_refl (ratAddRep x c))
                (ratLeRep_add_right (ratSubRep M c) x c hx))))
练习 5tendsToAtInfinity_congr定理附录解答

从某条线起逐点相等，极限相同

两个阈值 Mf 与 M0 用 exists_ge_both 并成 M；越过 M 的点同时越过两者，于是 f x 与 g x 相等，close 沿这个等式搬到 g 那一边。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_congr (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M0 : ratRep)
    (h : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M0 x → ratRel (f x) (g x))
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity g L :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 f 的分界线与「从 M0 起相等」的分界线并成一条 M；越过 M 的点同时越过两条，逐点用 close_wd_left 搬运。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity f L → (∀ x, M0 ≤ x → f x ~ g x) → TendsToAtInfinity g L。

提示 3关键件

exists_ge_both · ratLeRep_trans · close_wd_left · Exists.elim。

查看解答 定理 · 19 行
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_congr (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M0 : ratRep)
    (h : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M0 x → ratRel (f x) (g x))
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity g L :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    Exists.elim (hf eps heps)
      (fun (Mf : ratRep)
          (hMf : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mf x → close (f x) L eps) =>
        @Exists.elim ratRep
          (fun (M : ratRep) => ratLeRep Mf M ∧ ratLeRep M0 M)
          (∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
            close (g x) L eps)
          (exists_ge_both Mf M0)
          (fun (M : ratRep) (hM : ratLeRep Mf M ∧ ratLeRep M0 M) =>
            Exists.intro M
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLeRep M x) =>
                close_wd_left (f x) (g x) L eps
                  (h x (ratLeRep_trans M0 M x (And.right hM) hx))
                  (hMf x (ratLeRep_trans Mf M x (And.left hM) hx)))))
练习 6close_lt_add_right定理附录解答

从接近性读出一条严格上界

恒等函数的反例要用到：close x L ε 说明 x 不可能达到 L + ε。把接近性翻向，负侧给出 x - L < ε；两边加 L，左端用 (x - L) + L ~ x 收回，右端交换成 L + ε。

theorem close_lt_add_right (x L eps : ratRep) (h : close x L eps) :
    ratLtRep x (ratAddRep L eps) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先 close_symm，再用 close_neg_lt 得 x - L < ε；两边加 L，左端用 (x - L) + L ~ x 收回，右端用交换律。

提示 2目标形态

close x L ε → x < L + ε。

提示 3关键件

close_symm · close_neg_lt · ratLtRep_add_right · ratSubRep_add_self · ratAdd_comm。

查看解答 定理 · 13 行

把接近性翻向，负侧给出 x - L < ε；两边加 L，左端用 (x - L) + L ~ x 收回，右端交换成 L + ε。

theorem close_lt_add_right (x L eps : ratRep) (h : close x L eps) :
    ratLtRep x (ratAddRep L eps) :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd x
    (ratAddRep eps L)
    (ratAddRep L eps)
    (ratLtRep_congr_fst x
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x L) L)
      (ratAddRep eps L)
      (ratLtRep_add_right (ratSubRep x L) eps L
        (close_neg_lt L x eps (close_symm x L eps h)))
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x L) L) x
        (ratSubRep_add_self x L)))
    (ratAdd_comm eps L)
练习 7not_tendsToAtInfinity_id定理附录解答

恒等函数没有有限极限

反证：若恒等函数在无穷远处靠近 L，取 ε = 1，分界线 M。再取一个同时越过 M 与 L + 1 的 x。上一条给出 x < L + 1，而 x ≥ L + 1，两条序相撞。

theorem not_tendsToAtInfinity_id (L : ratRep) :
    ¬ TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => x) L :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

反证。取 ε = 1 得 M，再用 exists_ge_both 取同时越过 M 与 L + 1 的 x；上一条给 x < L + 1，与 x ≥ L + 1 夹出矛盾。

提示 2目标形态

¬ TendsToAtInfinity (fun x => x) L。

提示 3关键件

exists_ge_both · close_lt_add_right · ratLeRep_antisymm · ratLtRep_right。

查看解答 定理 · 25 行

反证：若恒等函数在无穷远处靠近 L，取 ε = 1，分界线 M。再取一个同时 越过 M 与 L + 1 的 x。close x L 1 给出 x < L + 1，而 x ≥ L + 1， 两条序相撞。

theorem not_tendsToAtInfinity_id (L : ratRep) :
    ¬ TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => x) L :=
  fun (h : TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => x) L) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (M : ratRep) => ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
        close x L ratOneRep)
      False
      (h ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)
      (fun (M : ratRep)
          (hM : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close x L ratOneRep) =>
        @Exists.elim ratRep
          (fun (x : ratRep) => ratLeRep M x ∧
            ratLeRep (ratAddRep L ratOneRep) x)
          False
          (exists_ge_both M (ratAddRep L ratOneRep))
          (fun (x : ratRep)
              (hx : ratLeRep M x ∧ ratLeRep (ratAddRep L ratOneRep) x) =>
            ratLtRep_right x (ratAddRep L ratOneRep)
              (close_lt_add_right x L ratOneRep (hM x (And.left hx)))
              (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep L ratOneRep) x
                (ratLeRep_antisymm (ratAddRep L ratOneRep) x
                  (And.right hx)
                  (ratLtRep_le x (ratAddRep L ratOneRep)
                    (close_lt_add_right x L ratOneRep
                      (hM x (And.left hx))))))))

无穷远处的极限：越过分界线之后靠近 L

7 道练习 · 7 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · tendsToAtInfinity_iff 定理

定义与展开是同一个命题，两个方向都直接返回假设。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep) :
    TendsToAtInfinity f L ↔
      ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
        ∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close (f x) L eps :=
  Iff.intro
    (fun (h : TendsToAtInfinity f L) => h)
    (fun (h : ∀ (eps : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps →
        ∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
          close (f x) L eps) => h)
练习 2 · exists_ge_both 定理

全性只给「a ≤ b 或 b ≤ a」两支；两支各自挑较大的那个当 M，另一条 弱序由传递性补上。

theorem exists_ge_both (a b : ratRep) :
    ∃ (M : ratRep), ratLeRep a M ∧ ratLeRep b M :=
  Or.elim
    (fun (hab : ratLeRep a b) =>
      Exists.intro b (And.intro hab (ratLeRep_refl b)))
    (fun (hba : ratLeRep b a) =>
      Exists.intro a (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a) hba))
    (ratLeRep_total a b)
练习 3 · tendsToAtInfinity_const 定理

常函数的输出与 L 相等，相等就落在每个正 ε 内；分界线取零即可。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_const (c : ratRep) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (_ : ratRep) => c) c :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    Exists.intro ratZeroRep
      (fun (x : ratRep) (_hx : ratLeRep ratZeroRep x) =>
        close_of_ratRel c c (ratRel_refl c) eps heps)
练习 4 · tendsToAtInfinity_shift 定理
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_shift (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep)
    (h : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => f (ratAddRep x c)) L :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    Exists.elim (h eps heps)
      (fun (M : ratRep)
          (hM : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close (f x) L eps) =>
        Exists.intro (ratSubRep M c)
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLeRep (ratSubRep M c) x) =>
            hM (ratAddRep x c)
              (ratLeRep_congr_gen
                (ratAddRep (ratSubRep M c) c) M
                (ratAddRep x c) (ratAddRep x c)
                (ratSubRep_add_self M c)
                (ratRel_refl (ratAddRep x c))
                (ratLeRep_add_right (ratSubRep M c) x c hx))))
练习 5 · tendsToAtInfinity_congr 定理
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_congr (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M0 : ratRep)
    (h : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M0 x → ratRel (f x) (g x))
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity g L :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    Exists.elim (hf eps heps)
      (fun (Mf : ratRep)
          (hMf : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mf x → close (f x) L eps) =>
        @Exists.elim ratRep
          (fun (M : ratRep) => ratLeRep Mf M ∧ ratLeRep M0 M)
          (∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
            close (g x) L eps)
          (exists_ge_both Mf M0)
          (fun (M : ratRep) (hM : ratLeRep Mf M ∧ ratLeRep M0 M) =>
            Exists.intro M
              (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLeRep M x) =>
                close_wd_left (f x) (g x) L eps
                  (h x (ratLeRep_trans M0 M x (And.right hM) hx))
                  (hMf x (ratLeRep_trans Mf M x (And.left hM) hx)))))
练习 6 · close_lt_add_right 定理

把接近性翻向，负侧给出 x - L < ε；两边加 L，左端用 (x - L) + L ~ x 收回，右端交换成 L + ε。

theorem close_lt_add_right (x L eps : ratRep) (h : close x L eps) :
    ratLtRep x (ratAddRep L eps) :=
  ratLtRep_congr_snd x
    (ratAddRep eps L)
    (ratAddRep L eps)
    (ratLtRep_congr_fst x
      (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x L) L)
      (ratAddRep eps L)
      (ratLtRep_add_right (ratSubRep x L) eps L
        (close_neg_lt L x eps (close_symm x L eps h)))
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x L) L) x
        (ratSubRep_add_self x L)))
    (ratAdd_comm eps L)
练习 7 · not_tendsToAtInfinity_id 定理

反证：若恒等函数在无穷远处靠近 L，取 ε = 1，分界线 M。再取一个同时 越过 M 与 L + 1 的 x。close x L 1 给出 x < L + 1，而 x ≥ L + 1， 两条序相撞。

theorem not_tendsToAtInfinity_id (L : ratRep) :
    ¬ TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => x) L :=
  fun (h : TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => x) L) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (M : ratRep) => ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
        close x L ratOneRep)
      False
      (h ratOneRep ratLtRep_zero_one)
      (fun (M : ratRep)
          (hM : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close x L ratOneRep) =>
        @Exists.elim ratRep
          (fun (x : ratRep) => ratLeRep M x ∧
            ratLeRep (ratAddRep L ratOneRep) x)
          False
          (exists_ge_both M (ratAddRep L ratOneRep))
          (fun (x : ratRep)
              (hx : ratLeRep M x ∧ ratLeRep (ratAddRep L ratOneRep) x) =>
            ratLtRep_right x (ratAddRep L ratOneRep)
              (close_lt_add_right x L ratOneRep (hM x (And.left hx)))
              (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep L ratOneRep) x
                (ratLeRep_antisymm (ratAddRep L ratOneRep) x
                  (And.right hx)
                  (ratLtRep_le x (ratAddRep L ratOneRep)
                    (close_lt_add_right x L ratOneRep
                      (hM x (And.left hx))))))))

III.6 · 本章第 15 / 16 单元 · 全书第 138 / 171 单元

138无穷远处极限的代数与序列判据

Unit 138 — Algebra of Limits at Infinity and the Sequential Criterion

1 个定义 · 7 个定理 · 7 道练习 · 21 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

上一单元只立了定义，能算的例子还只有常函数与平移。若两个函数各自在无穷远处靠近 L 与 M，它们的和是否靠近 L + M？把 x 一直往右推，两个误差都变小，答案应当是肯定的。证明与一点处的和律同形：ε 折半，两个分界线用序的全性合成一条，越过它之后两条接近性同时成立。

取负、减法、加常数都是同一条和律的变形。真正把「无穷远处的极限」接回序列语言的是下面这条判据：如果自变量序列 x_n 最终越过任何一条分界线，那么 f(x_n) 就收敛到 L。它说明这里的极限仍然是 ε-N 式的极限，只不过阈值从下标换成了数轴上的位置。

边界：函数取 ratRep → ratRep，序列取 Nat → ratRep，都停在有理代表元层；不把 ±∞ 当成数，也不讨论函数值趋于无穷。不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。

序列趋于无穷

读法：无论把分界线 M 放在哪里，序列最终都越过它。这里不说「极限是无穷大」，只说「最终超过每个给定的有理数」，避免引入新的极限对象。

定义TendsToInfinity声明 1
def TendsToInfinity (x : Seq) : Prop :=
  ∀ (M : ratRep), ∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → ratLeRep M (x n)
练习 1tendsToInfinity_iff定理附录解答
theorem tendsToInfinity_iff (x : Seq) :
    TendsToInfinity x ↔
      ∀ (M : ratRep), ∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n →
        ratLeRep M (x n) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 TendsToInfinity 展开，右边那串量词逐字就是定义；两个方向都直接返回假设。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToInfinity x ↔ ∀ M, ∃ N, ∀ n, N ≤ n → M ≤ x n。

提示 3关键件

Iff.intro · 定义性展开。

查看解答 定理 · 7 行

定义与展开是同一个命题。

theorem tendsToInfinity_iff (x : Seq) :
    TendsToInfinity x ↔
      ∀ (M : ratRep), ∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n →
        ratLeRep M (x n) :=
  Iff.intro (fun (h : TendsToInfinity x) => h)
    (fun (h : ∀ (M : ratRep), ∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n →
        ratLeRep M (x n)) => h)
练习 2tendsToAtInfinity_add定理附录解答

和的极限

两个 ε/2 的界分别由 f 与 g 给出；两条分界线并成 K，越过 K 的点同时越过两条原线。close_add 把两个一半的误差合成 ε。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hg : TendsToAtInfinity g M) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratAddRep L M) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 ε 取半数，分别向 f、g 的极限要分界线；用 exists_ge_both 并成 K，越过 K 的点同时越过两条原线，最后 close_add 合成。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity f L → TendsToAtInfinity g M → TendsToAtInfinity (fun x => f x + g x) (L + M)。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_half · exists_ge_both · ratLeRep_trans · close_add。

查看解答 定理 · 35 行
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hg : TendsToAtInfinity g M) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratAddRep L M) :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (Mf : ratRep) => ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mf x →
        close (f x) L (ratHalf eps))
      (∃ (K : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep K x →
        close (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep L M) eps)
      (hf (ratHalf eps) (ratLtRep_half eps heps))
      (fun (Mf : ratRep)
          (hMf : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mf x →
            close (f x) L (ratHalf eps)) =>
        @Exists.elim ratRep
          (fun (Mg : ratRep) => ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mg x →
            close (g x) M (ratHalf eps))
          (∃ (K : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep K x →
            close (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep L M) eps)
          (hg (ratHalf eps) (ratLtRep_half eps heps))
          (fun (Mg : ratRep)
              (hMg : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mg x →
                close (g x) M (ratHalf eps)) =>
            @Exists.elim ratRep
              (fun (K : ratRep) => ratLeRep Mf K ∧ ratLeRep Mg K)
              (∃ (K : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep K x →
                close (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep L M) eps)
              (exists_ge_both Mf Mg)
              (fun (K : ratRep)
                  (hK : ratLeRep Mf K ∧ ratLeRep Mg K) =>
                Exists.intro K
                  (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLeRep K x) =>
                    close_add (f x) L (g x) M eps
                      (hMf x (ratLeRep_trans Mf K x (And.left hK) hx))
                      (hMg x (ratLeRep_trans Mg K x (And.right hK) hx))))))
练习 3tendsToAtInfinity_neg定理附录解答

取负

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_neg (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (f x))
      (ratNegRep L) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

分界线不变，逐点用 close_neg 把误差搬到相反数上。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity f L → TendsToAtInfinity (fun x => -f x) (-L)。

提示 3关键件

close_neg · 定义性展开。

查看解答 定理 · 16 行

分界线不变，逐点用 close_neg 把误差搬到相反数上。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_neg (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (f x))
      (ratNegRep L) :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (M : ratRep) => ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
        close (f x) L eps)
      (∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
        close (ratNegRep (f x)) (ratNegRep L) eps)
      (hf eps heps)
      (fun (M : ratRep)
          (hM : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close (f x) L eps) =>
        Exists.intro M
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLeRep M x) =>
            close_neg (f x) L eps (hM x hx)))
练习 4tendsToAtInfinity_sub定理附录解答

减法与加常数

减法就是加负元：先取负，再交给和律；两个结论函数定义性相同。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hg : TendsToAtInfinity g M) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratSubRep L M) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 f - g 读成 f + (-g)，先对 g 用取负律，再交给和律。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity (fun x => f x - g x) (L - M)。

提示 3关键件

tendsToAtInfinity_add · tendsToAtInfinity_neg。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hg : TendsToAtInfinity g M) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratSubRep L M) :=
  tendsToAtInfinity_add f (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (g x)) L
    (ratNegRep M) hf (tendsToAtInfinity_neg g M hg)
练习 5tendsToAtInfinity_add_const定理附录解答

常数项不随 x 变，直接当作常函数交给和律。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_add_const (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) c)
      (ratAddRep L c) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把常数 c 看成常函数，它的极限是 c，再与 f 走和律。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity f L → TendsToAtInfinity (fun x => f x + c) (L + c)。

提示 3关键件

tendsToAtInfinity_add · tendsToAtInfinity_const。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_add_const (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) c)
      (ratAddRep L c) :=
  tendsToAtInfinity_add f (fun (_ : ratRep) => c) L c hf
    (tendsToAtInfinity_const c)
练习 6tendsToAtInfinity_comp定理附录解答

序列判据：把自变量换成一条趋于无穷的序列

给定 ε，f 的极限给出一条分界线 M；序列的假设对同一条 M 给出阈值 N。N 之后每个 x n 都越过 M，于是 f (x n) 落在 ε 界内。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_comp (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep)
    (x : Seq) (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hx : TendsToInfinity x) :
    Converges (fun (n : Nat) => f (x n)) L :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

给定 ε 得 M；把 M 交给序列假设得阈值 N；N 之后逐项用 f 的极限。

提示 2目标形态

TendsToAtInfinity f L → TendsToInfinity x → Converges (fun n => f (x n)) L。

提示 3关键件

TendsToInfinity 的展开 · Converges 的展开 · Exists.elim。

查看解答 定理 · 20 行
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_comp (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep)
    (x : Seq) (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hx : TendsToInfinity x) :
    Converges (fun (n : Nat) => f (x n)) L :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (M : ratRep) => ∀ (y : ratRep), ratLeRep M y →
        close (f y) L eps)
      (∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → close (f (x n)) L eps)
      (hf eps heps)
      (fun (M : ratRep)
          (hM : ∀ (y : ratRep), ratLeRep M y → close (f y) L eps) =>
        @Exists.elim Nat
          (fun (N : Nat) => ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → ratLeRep M (x n))
          (∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → close (f (x n)) L eps)
          (hx M)
          (fun (N : Nat)
              (hN : ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → ratLeRep M (x n)) =>
            Exists.intro N
              (fun (n : Nat) (hn : LeNat N n) =>
                hM (x n) (hN n hn))))
练习 7demo_comp_add_const定理附录解答

具体：和的像仍然收敛

把「加常数」与序列判据串起来：f (x n) + c 收敛到 L + c。

theorem demo_comp_add_const (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep) (x : Seq)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hx : TendsToInfinity x) :
    Converges (fun (n : Nat) => ratAddRep (f (x n)) c)
      (ratAddRep L c) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 fun y => f y + c 用加常数律得到它在无穷远处的极限，再交给序列判据。

提示 2目标形态

Converges (fun n => f (x n) + c) (L + c)。

提示 3关键件

tendsToAtInfinity_add_const · tendsToAtInfinity_comp。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行
theorem demo_comp_add_const (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep) (x : Seq)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hx : TendsToInfinity x) :
    Converges (fun (n : Nat) => ratAddRep (f (x n)) c)
      (ratAddRep L c) :=
  tendsToAtInfinity_comp (fun (y : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f y) c)
    (ratAddRep L c) x (tendsToAtInfinity_add_const f c L hf) hx

无穷远处极限的代数与序列判据

7 道练习 · 7 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · tendsToInfinity_iff 定理

定义与展开是同一个命题。

theorem tendsToInfinity_iff (x : Seq) :
    TendsToInfinity x ↔
      ∀ (M : ratRep), ∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n →
        ratLeRep M (x n) :=
  Iff.intro (fun (h : TendsToInfinity x) => h)
    (fun (h : ∀ (M : ratRep), ∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n →
        ratLeRep M (x n)) => h)
练习 2 · tendsToAtInfinity_add 定理
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_add (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hg : TendsToAtInfinity g M) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratAddRep L M) :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (Mf : ratRep) => ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mf x →
        close (f x) L (ratHalf eps))
      (∃ (K : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep K x →
        close (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep L M) eps)
      (hf (ratHalf eps) (ratLtRep_half eps heps))
      (fun (Mf : ratRep)
          (hMf : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mf x →
            close (f x) L (ratHalf eps)) =>
        @Exists.elim ratRep
          (fun (Mg : ratRep) => ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mg x →
            close (g x) M (ratHalf eps))
          (∃ (K : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep K x →
            close (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep L M) eps)
          (hg (ratHalf eps) (ratLtRep_half eps heps))
          (fun (Mg : ratRep)
              (hMg : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep Mg x →
                close (g x) M (ratHalf eps)) =>
            @Exists.elim ratRep
              (fun (K : ratRep) => ratLeRep Mf K ∧ ratLeRep Mg K)
              (∃ (K : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep K x →
                close (ratAddRep (f x) (g x)) (ratAddRep L M) eps)
              (exists_ge_both Mf Mg)
              (fun (K : ratRep)
                  (hK : ratLeRep Mf K ∧ ratLeRep Mg K) =>
                Exists.intro K
                  (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLeRep K x) =>
                    close_add (f x) L (g x) M eps
                      (hMf x (ratLeRep_trans Mf K x (And.left hK) hx))
                      (hMg x (ratLeRep_trans Mg K x (And.right hK) hx))))))
练习 3 · tendsToAtInfinity_neg 定理

分界线不变，逐点用 close_neg 把误差搬到相反数上。

theorem tendsToAtInfinity_neg (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (f x))
      (ratNegRep L) :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (M : ratRep) => ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
        close (f x) L eps)
      (∃ (M : ratRep), ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x →
        close (ratNegRep (f x)) (ratNegRep L) eps)
      (hf eps heps)
      (fun (M : ratRep)
          (hM : ∀ (x : ratRep), ratLeRep M x → close (f x) L eps) =>
        Exists.intro M
          (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : ratLeRep M x) =>
            close_neg (f x) L eps (hM x hx)))
练习 4 · tendsToAtInfinity_sub 定理
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_sub (f g : ratRep → ratRep) (L M : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hg : TendsToAtInfinity g M) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratSubRep (f x) (g x))
      (ratSubRep L M) :=
  tendsToAtInfinity_add f (fun (x : ratRep) => ratNegRep (g x)) L
    (ratNegRep M) hf (tendsToAtInfinity_neg g M hg)
练习 5 · tendsToAtInfinity_add_const 定理
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_add_const (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) :
    TendsToAtInfinity (fun (x : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f x) c)
      (ratAddRep L c) :=
  tendsToAtInfinity_add f (fun (_ : ratRep) => c) L c hf
    (tendsToAtInfinity_const c)
练习 6 · tendsToAtInfinity_comp 定理
theorem tendsToAtInfinity_comp (f : ratRep → ratRep) (L : ratRep)
    (x : Seq) (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hx : TendsToInfinity x) :
    Converges (fun (n : Nat) => f (x n)) L :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (M : ratRep) => ∀ (y : ratRep), ratLeRep M y →
        close (f y) L eps)
      (∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → close (f (x n)) L eps)
      (hf eps heps)
      (fun (M : ratRep)
          (hM : ∀ (y : ratRep), ratLeRep M y → close (f y) L eps) =>
        @Exists.elim Nat
          (fun (N : Nat) => ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → ratLeRep M (x n))
          (∃ (N : Nat), ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → close (f (x n)) L eps)
          (hx M)
          (fun (N : Nat)
              (hN : ∀ (n : Nat), LeNat N n → ratLeRep M (x n)) =>
            Exists.intro N
              (fun (n : Nat) (hn : LeNat N n) =>
                hM (x n) (hN n hn))))
练习 7 · demo_comp_add_const 定理
theorem demo_comp_add_const (f : ratRep → ratRep) (c L : ratRep) (x : Seq)
    (hf : TendsToAtInfinity f L) (hx : TendsToInfinity x) :
    Converges (fun (n : Nat) => ratAddRep (f (x n)) c)
      (ratAddRep L c) :=
  tendsToAtInfinity_comp (fun (y : ratRep) => ratAddRep (f y) c)
    (ratAddRep L c) x (tendsToAtInfinity_add_const f c L hf) hx

III.6 · 本章第 16 / 16 单元 · 全书第 139 / 171 单元

139单调函数：介值性反过来给出连续

Unit 139 — Monotone Functions: Intermediate Values Force Continuity

2 个定义 · 24 个定理 · 13 道练习 · 39 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

134 到 136 用连续性换来了最值与介值：连续函数在闭区间上取到最值，从一个负值走到正值时必经过零。这些结论都从连续性出发。反过来的问题同样值得问：「不跳过中间值」这一条性质，能不能把连续性认出来？

单调函数是回答这个问题最省的对象。它的图像只朝一个方向走，所以不连续的方式只剩一种：在某个点跳过去，把一段区间里的值整段让开。若它从不跳过任何一个中间值，就无处可跳，只能连续。ℚ 上的连续函数未必有介值性（x² - 2 在 [1,2] 上连续却没有有理零点），所以这里的介值性是一条显式假设，不是自动成立的事实。

证明把连续性拆成两个单侧的上界。给定 eps，在 c 的右边找一个分界点 u，使 c 到 u 之间的函数值都不超过 f c + eps/2。若右端点的值本来就不超过这个阈值，右端点本身就是 u；否则介值性在 c 与 b 之间给出一个点，它的值正好是阈值。左边对称地得到 l。δ 取 u - c 与 c - l 中较小的那个，于是 c 的 δ 邻域被夹在 [l, u] 里，两次单侧估计合起来就是 |f x - f c| < eps。

边界：函数取 ratRep → ratRep，停在有理代表元层；介值性作为假设出现，因为它在 ℚ 上不是连续性的推论。不新增公理，也不碰 kernel/front。

定义：严格递增与介值性

严格递增只看严格序：x < y 时 f x < f y。它不假设函数尊重代表元的等价，所以这条性质只在不跨越等价类的地方使用，构造单射时正好够用。

定义StrictlyIncreasing声明 1
def StrictlyIncreasing (f : ratRep → ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x y : ratRep), ratLtRep x y → ratLtRep (f x) (f y)

介值性把「不跳过中间值」写成一个量词句：区间里两点之间取到的每个值，都被某个严格的中间点取到。函数停在代表元层，取到的值只能报到等价类，所以结论写成 ratRel (f c) v 而不是相等。

定义HasIntermediateValue声明 2
def HasIntermediateValue (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x y : ratRep), InClosed a b x → InClosed a b y → ratLtRep x y →
    ∀ (v : ratRep), ratLtRep (f x) v → ratLtRep v (f y) →
      ∃ (c : ratRep), ratLtRep x c ∧ ratLtRep c y ∧ ratRel (f c) v

定义的展开

定义与展开是同一个命题，两个方向都直接返回假设。

定理strictlyIncreasing_iff声明 3
theorem strictlyIncreasing_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) :
    StrictlyIncreasing f ↔
      ∀ (x y : ratRep), ratLtRep x y → ratLtRep (f x) (f y) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 2 行
  1.   Iff.intro (fun (h : StrictlyIncreasing f) => h)
    (fun (h : ∀ (x y : ratRep), ratLtRep x y → ratLtRep (f x) (f y)) => h)

同一个定义换一行写，展开后逐字相同。

定理hasIntermediateValue_iff声明 4
theorem hasIntermediateValue_iff (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep) :
    HasIntermediateValue f a b ↔
      ∀ (x y : ratRep), InClosed a b x → InClosed a b y → ratLtRep x y →
        ∀ (v : ratRep), ratLtRep (f x) v → ratLtRep v (f y) →
          ∃ (c : ratRep), ratLtRep x c ∧ ratLtRep c y ∧ ratRel (f c) v :=
证明 · 1 步 · 5 行
  1.   Iff.intro (fun (h : HasIntermediateValue f a b) => h)
    (fun (h : ∀ (x y : ratRep), InClosed a b x → InClosed a b y →
        ratLtRep x y → ∀ (v : ratRep), ratLtRep (f x) v →
          ratLtRep v (f y) → ∃ (c : ratRep),
            ratLtRep x c ∧ ratLtRep c y ∧ ratRel (f c) v) => h)
练习 1strictlyIncreasing_injective定理附录解答

单射

严格递增把不同的点推开：f x 与 f y 等价时，x 与 y 必须等价。证明按序的全性分左右两支；每支再用「弱序或者相等、或者严格小」拆开，严格小的那一支与已知的等价直接冲突。

theorem strictlyIncreasing_injective (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hf : StrictlyIncreasing f) (x y : ratRep) (h : ratRel (f x) (f y)) :
    ratRel x y :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 ratLeRep_total x y 分两支；每支用 ratLeRep_eq_or_lt 拆成相等或严格小，严格小的一支把 hf 给出的严格不等式与 h 摆在一起，用 ratLtRep_right 收尾。

提示 2目标形态

StrictlyIncreasing f → ratRel (f x) (f y) → ratRel x y。

提示 3关键件

ratLeRep_total · ratLeRep_eq_or_lt · ratLtRep_right · False.elim · ratRel_symm。

查看解答 定理 · 18 行
theorem strictlyIncreasing_injective (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hf : StrictlyIncreasing f) (x y : ratRep) (h : ratRel (f x) (f y)) :
    ratRel x y :=
  @Or.elim (ratLeRep x y) (ratLeRep y x) (ratRel x y)
    (fun (hxy : ratLeRep x y) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel x y) (ratLtRep x y) (ratRel x y)
        (fun (heq : ratRel x y) => heq)
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep x y) =>
          False.elim (ratLtRep_right (f x) (f y) (hf x y hlt) h))
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt x y hxy))
    (fun (hyx : ratLeRep y x) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel y x) (ratLtRep y x) (ratRel x y)
        (fun (heq : ratRel y x) => ratRel_symm y x heq)
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep y x) =>
          False.elim (ratLtRep_right (f y) (f x) (hf y x hlt)
            (ratRel_symm (f x) (f y) h)))
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt y x hyx))
    (ratLeRep_total x y)

序与差的小工具

严格序在左边加同一个数也稳定。先对右边加，再用交换律把两端的次序搬回来。

定理ratLtRep_add_left声明 6
theorem ratLtRep_add_left (x y z : ratRep) (h : ratLtRep x y) :
    ratLtRep (ratAddRep z x) (ratAddRep z y) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 4 行
  1.   ratLtRep_congr_snd (ratAddRep z x) (ratAddRep y z) (ratAddRep z y)
    (ratLtRep_congr_fst (ratAddRep z x) (ratAddRep x z) (ratAddRep y z)
      (ratLtRep_add_right x y z h) (ratAdd_comm z x))
    (ratAdd_comm y z)

弱序换个说法：x ≤ y 就是 0 ≤ y - x。两边同加 -x 得 -y ≤ -x，再在左边加上 y，把 y + (-y) 收成零。

定理le_sub_of_le声明 7
theorem le_sub_of_le (x y : ratRep) (h : ratLeRep x y) :
    ratLeRep ratZeroRep (ratSubRep y x) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 5 行
  1.   ratLeRep_congr_gen
    (ratAddRep y (ratNegRep y)) ratZeroRep
    (ratSubRep y x) (ratSubRep y x)
    (ratAdd_neg_gen y) (ratRel_refl (ratSubRep y x))
    (ratLeRep_add_left (ratNegRep y) (ratNegRep x) y (ratLeRep_neg_gen x y h))

上一条的逆用：x - y ≤ d 时两边加 y，得到 x ≤ y + d；左边用 (x - y) + y ~ x 收回。

定理le_add_of_sub_le声明 8
theorem le_add_of_sub_le (x y d : ratRep)
    (h : ratLeRep (ratSubRep x y) d) : ratLeRep x (ratAddRep y d) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 6 行
  1.   ratLeRep_trans x (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x y) y) (ratAddRep y d)
    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel x (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x y) y)
      (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x y) y) x (ratSubRep_add_self x y)))
    (ratLeRep_trans (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x y) y) (ratAddRep d y) (ratAddRep y d)
      (ratLeRep_add_right (ratSubRep x y) d y h)
      (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep d y) (ratAddRep y d) (ratAdd_comm d y)))

两边同时减去同一个数不改变弱序：把 c - x ≤ c - l 的公共被减数 c 消掉，得到 -x ≤ -l，再取负换成 l ≤ x。

定理le_of_sub_right_le声明 9
theorem le_of_sub_right_le (c x l : ratRep)
    (h : ratLeRep (ratSubRep c x) (ratSubRep c l)) : ratLeRep l x :=
证明 · 1 步 · 11 行
  1.   ratLeRep_congr_gen (ratNegRep (ratNegRep l)) l
    (ratNegRep (ratNegRep x)) x
    (ratNegRep_invol l) (ratNegRep_invol x)
    (ratLeRep_neg_gen (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep l)
      (ratLeRep_add_cancel_gen (ratNegRep x) (ratNegRep l) c
        (ratLeRep_congr_gen
          (ratSubRep c x) (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) c)
          (ratSubRep c l) (ratAddRep (ratNegRep l) c)
          (ratAdd_comm c (ratNegRep x))
          (ratAdd_comm c (ratNegRep l))
          h)))

减去一个正数会变小：x - h 加回 h 得到 x，而加正数严格抬大。

定理ratLtRep_self_sub_pos声明 10
theorem ratLtRep_self_sub_pos (x h : ratRep) (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ratLtRep (ratSubRep x h) x :=
证明 · 1 步 · 3 行
  1.   ratLtRep_congr_snd (ratSubRep x h) (ratAddRep (ratSubRep x h) h) x
    (ratLtRep_self_add_pos (ratSubRep x h) h hh)
    (ratSubRep_add_self x h)

两个正数里有较小的一个。序的全性给出两支，每支取自己那一端；它不超过另一个则由全性直接给出。

定理exists_min_pos声明 11
theorem exists_min_pos (p q : ratRep) (hp : ratLtRep ratZeroRep p)
    (hq : ratLtRep ratZeroRep q) :
    ∃ (d : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep d ∧ ratLeRep d p ∧ ratLeRep d q :=
证明 · 1 步 · 7 行
  1.   @Or.elim (ratLeRep p q) (ratLeRep q p)
    (∃ (d : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep d ∧ ratLeRep d p ∧ ratLeRep d q)
    (fun (h : ratLeRep p q) =>
      Exists.intro p (And.intro hp (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl p) h)))
    (fun (h : ratLeRep q p) =>
      Exists.intro q (And.intro hq (And.intro h (ratLeRep_refl q))))
    (ratLeRep_total p q)

close 的两侧收口

单侧估计变成 close：L ≤ x 且 x < L + eps 时，|x - L| 就是 x - L，它小于 eps。见证取 x - L 本身，绝对值那一支由非负性给出；严格界通过 (L + eps) - L ~ eps 搬到 eps 上。

定理close_of_le_lt声明 12
theorem close_of_le_lt (x L eps : ratRep)
    (h1 : ratLeRep L x) (h2 : ratLtRep x (ratAddRep L eps)) :
    close x L eps :=
证明 · 1 步 · 25 行
  1.   Exists.intro (ratSubRep x L)
    (And.intro
      (Or.inl (And.intro (le_sub_of_le L x h1)
        (ratRel_refl (ratSubRep x L))))
      (ratLtRep_congr_snd (ratSubRep x L)
        (ratAddRep (ratAddRep L eps) (ratNegRep L)) eps
        (ratLtRep_add_right x (ratAddRep L eps) (ratNegRep L) h2)
        (ratRel_trans_gen
          (ratAddRep (ratAddRep L eps) (ratNegRep L))
          (ratAddRep (ratAddRep eps L) (ratNegRep L))
          eps
          (ratAddRep_wd_gen (ratAddRep L eps) (ratAddRep eps L)
            (ratNegRep L) (ratAdd_comm L eps))
          (ratRel_trans_gen
            (ratAddRep (ratAddRep eps L) (ratNegRep L))
            (ratAddRep eps (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)))
            eps
            (ratAdd_assoc eps L (ratNegRep L))
            (ratRel_trans_gen
              (ratAddRep eps (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L)))
              (ratAddRep eps ratZeroRep)
              eps
              (ratAddRep_wd_snd_gen eps (ratAddRep L (ratNegRep L))
                ratZeroRep (ratAdd_neg_gen L))
              (ratAdd_zero eps))))))

两个条件合成 close：L ≤ x ≤ L + h 且 h < eps。先把上界放大成 x < L + eps，再交给上一条。

定理close_of_le_ub声明 13
theorem close_of_le_ub (x L eps h : ratRep)
    (h1 : ratLeRep L x) (h2 : ratLeRep x (ratAddRep L h))
    (hh : ratLtRep h eps) : close x L eps :=
证明 · 1 步 · 3 行
  1.   close_of_le_lt x L eps h1
    (ratLtRep_of_le_lt x (ratAddRep L h) (ratAddRep L eps) h2
      (ratLtRep_add_left h eps L hh))

下界那一侧同样处理，只是最后要把 close 的方向翻过来。

定理close_of_le_lb声明 14
theorem close_of_le_lb (x L eps h : ratRep)
    (h1 : ratLeRep x L) (h2 : ratLeRep L (ratAddRep x h))
    (hh : ratLtRep h eps) : close x L eps :=
证明 · 1 步 · 4 行
  1.   close_symm L x eps
    (close_of_le_lt L x eps h1
      (ratLtRep_of_le_lt L (ratAddRep x h) (ratAddRep x eps) h2
        (ratLtRep_add_left h eps x hh)))
练习 2right_bound_flat定理附录解答

右分界点

右端点的值已经在阈值以下时，右端点本身就是分界点：区间里任何 x ≤ b 都有 f x ≤ f b ≤ f c + h。

theorem right_bound_flat (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hfb : ratLeRep (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

取 u = b；对 x ≤ b 用单调性把 f x 压到 f b 之下，再接上 f b ≤ f c + h。

提示 2目标形态

∃ u, c < u ∧ u ≤ b ∧ ∀ x ∈ [a,b], c ≤ x → x ≤ u → f x ≤ f c + h，取 u = b。

提示 3关键件

Exists.intro · And.intro · ratLeRep_trans · ratLeRep_refl · NondecreasingOn 的展开。

查看解答 定理 · 19 行
theorem right_bound_flat (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hfb : ratLeRep (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  Exists.intro b
    (And.intro hcb
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl b)
        (fun (x : ratRep) (hIn : InClosed a b x) (_hcx : ratLeRep c x)
            (hxb : ratLeRep x b) =>
          ratLeRep_trans (f x) (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)
            (hmono x b hIn
              (And.intro
                (ratLtRep_le a b (ratLtRep_trans a c b hac hcb))
                (ratLeRep_refl b))
              hxb)
            hfb)))
练习 3right_bound_of_lt定理附录解答

右端点的值超过阈值时，介值性在 c 与 b 之间给出一个点 u，它的值恰好是阈值。u 严格在 c 的右边（阈值严格大于 f c），也严格在 b 的左边（阈值严格小于 f b），于是 c 到 u 之间的函数值都被阈值压住。

theorem right_bound_of_lt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h)
    (hlt : ratLtRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 x = c、y = b、v = f c + h 交给介值性；它给出 c < u < b 且 f u ~ f c + h，再对 x ≤ u 用单调性。

提示 2目标形态

与 right_bound_flat 相同的存在句。

提示 3关键件

hivp · ratLtRep_self_add_pos · ratLtRep_le · ratLeRep_of_ratRel · ratLeRep_trans。

theorem right_bound_of_lt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h)
    (hlt : ratLtRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  @Exists.elim ratRep
    (fun (v : ratRep) => ratLtRep c v ∧ ratLtRep v b ∧
      ratRel (f v) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (hivp c b
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a c hac) (ratLtRep_le c b hcb))
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a b (ratLtRep_trans a c b hac hcb))
        (ratLeRep_refl b))
      hcb (ratAddRep (f c) h)
      (ratLtRep_self_add_pos (f c) h hh) hlt)
    (fun (u : ratRep)
        (hu : ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLtRep u b ∧
          ratRel (f u) (ratAddRep (f c) h)) =>
      Exists.intro u
        (And.intro (And.left hu)
          (And.intro (ratLtRep_le u b (And.left (And.right hu)))
            (fun (x : ratRep) (hIn : InClosed a b x) (hcx : ratLeRep c x)
                (hxu : ratLeRep x u) =>
              ratLeRep_trans (f x) (f u) (ratAddRep (f c) h)
                (hmono x u hIn
                  (And.intro
                    (ratLeRep_trans a c u (ratLtRep_le a c hac)
                      (ratLtRep_le c u (And.left hu)))
                    (ratLtRep_le u b (And.left (And.right hu))))
                  hxu)
                (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (f u) (ratAddRep (f c) h)
                  (And.right (And.right hu)))))))
练习 4exists_right_bound定理附录解答

两支接起来。按 f b 与 f c + h 的序分；第二支里还要用「弱序或者相等、或者严格小」把相等的边界并入平的那一支。

theorem exists_right_bound (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 ratLeRep (f b) (f c + h) 分两支；第二支再用 ratLeRep_eq_or_lt 拆相等或严格小，相等交给 right_bound_flat，严格小交给 right_bound_of_lt。

theorem exists_right_bound (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  @Or.elim (ratLeRep (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (ratLeRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b))
    (∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (fun (hle : ratLeRep (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)) =>
      right_bound_flat f a b c h hmono hac hcb hle)
    (fun (hge : ratLeRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b))
        (ratLtRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b))
        (∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
            ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
        (fun (heq : ratRel (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) =>
          right_bound_flat f a b c h hmono hac hcb
            (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)
              (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b) heq)))
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) =>
          right_bound_of_lt f a b c h hmono hivp hac hcb hh hlt)
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b) hge))
    (ratLeRep_total (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
练习 5left_bound_flat定理附录解答

左分界点

左端点已经在阈值以上时，取 l = a：区间里 x ≥ a 有 f x ≥ f a ≥ f c - h，于是 f c ≤ f x + h。

theorem left_bound_flat (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hfa : ratLeRep (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a)) :
    ∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

取 l = a；对 x ≥ a 用单调性把 f a 抬到 f x 之下，再用 le_add_of_sub_le 把 f c - h ≤ f x 换成 f c ≤ h + f x，最后交换加数。

提示 2目标形态

∃ l, l < c ∧ a ≤ l ∧ ∀ x ∈ [a,b], l ≤ x → x ≤ c → f c ≤ f x + h，取 l = a。

提示 3关键件

Exists.intro · le_add_of_sub_le · ratAdd_comm · ratLeRep_congr_gen。

查看解答 定理 · 22 行
theorem left_bound_flat (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hfa : ratLeRep (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a)) :
    ∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h) :=
  Exists.intro a
    (And.intro hac
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a)
        (fun (x : ratRep) (hIn : InClosed a b x) (hax : ratLeRep a x)
            (hxc : ratLeRep x c) =>
          ratLeRep_congr_gen (f c) (f c)
            (ratAddRep h (f x)) (ratAddRep (f x) h)
            (ratRel_refl (f c)) (ratAdd_comm h (f x))
            (le_add_of_sub_le (f c) h (f x)
              (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a) (f x) hfa
                (hmono a x
                  (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a)
                    (ratLeRep_trans a c b (ratLtRep_le a c hac)
                      (ratLtRep_le c b hcb)))
                  hIn hax))))))
练习 6left_bound_of_lt定理附录解答

左端点的值低于阈值时，介值性在 a 与 c 之间给出 l，它的值恰好是阈值。l 严格在 a 的右边、严格在 c 的左边（阈值严格小于 f c），于是 l 到 c 之间的函数值都在阈值以上。

theorem left_bound_of_lt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h)
    (hlt : ratLtRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) :
    ∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

把 x = a、y = c、v = f c - h 交给介值性；它给出 a < l < c 且 f l ~ f c - h，再对 x ≥ l 用单调性得到 f l ≤ f x。

theorem left_bound_of_lt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h)
    (hlt : ratLtRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) :
    ∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h) :=
  @Exists.elim ratRep
    (fun (v : ratRep) => ratLtRep a v ∧ ratLtRep v c ∧
      ratRel (f v) (ratSubRep (f c) h))
    (∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
    (hivp a c
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a)
        (ratLtRep_le a b (ratLtRep_trans a c b hac hcb)))
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a c hac) (ratLtRep_le c b hcb))
      hac (ratSubRep (f c) h) hlt (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos (f c) h hh))
    (fun (l : ratRep)
        (hl : ratLtRep a l ∧ ratLtRep l c ∧
          ratRel (f l) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) =>
      Exists.intro l
        (And.intro (And.left (And.right hl))
          (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a l (And.left hl))
            (fun (x : ratRep) (hIn : InClosed a b x) (hlx : ratLeRep l x)
                (hxc : ratLeRep x c) =>
              ratLeRep_congr_gen (f c) (f c)
                (ratAddRep h (f x)) (ratAddRep (f x) h)
                (ratRel_refl (f c)) (ratAdd_comm h (f x))
                (le_add_of_sub_le (f c) h (f x)
                  (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f l) (f x)
                    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f l)
                      (ratRel_symm (f l) (ratSubRep (f c) h)
                        (And.right (And.right hl))))
                    (hmono l x
                      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a l (And.left hl))
                        (ratLeRep_trans l c b
                          (ratLtRep_le l c (And.left (And.right hl)))
                          (ratLtRep_le c b hcb)))
                      hIn hlx)))))))

theorem exists_left_bound (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h) :=
  @Or.elim (ratLeRep (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a))
    (ratLeRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h))
    (∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
    (fun (hfa : ratLeRep (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a)) =>
      left_bound_flat f a b c h hmono hac hcb hfa)
    (fun (haf : ratLeRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h))
        (ratLtRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h))

          ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
            ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
        (fun (heq : ratRel (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) =>
          left_bound_flat f a b c h hmono hac hcb
            (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a)
              (ratRel_symm (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h) heq)))

          left_bound_of_lt f a b c h hmono hivp hac hcb hh hlt)

练习 8right_close_of_delta定理附录解答

δ 邻域上的收口与主定理

右半邻域上的收口：x 在 c 的右边、且 |x - c| < δ ≤ u - c 时，x 落在 [c, u] 里，于是 f c ≤ f x ≤ f c + h < f c + eps。

theorem right_close_of_delta (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (a b c u h eps delta : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hub : ratLeRep u b)
    (hbound : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x →
      ratLeRep x u → ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (hdu : ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep u c))
    (hhe : ratLtRep h eps)
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x c delta) (hcx : ratLeRep c x) :
    close (f x) (f c) eps :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

从 close_two_bounds 取出 x - c ≤ δ，用 le_add_of_sub_le 得 x ≤ c + δ，再经 δ ≤ u - c 把右边认成 u；最后交给 close_of_le_ub。

提示 2目标形态

close x c δ → c ≤ x → close (f x) (f c) eps。

提示 3关键件

close_two_bounds · le_add_of_sub_le · ratLeRep_add_left · ratSubRep_add_self · close_of_le_ub。

theorem right_close_of_delta (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (a b c u h eps delta : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hub : ratLeRep u b)
    (hbound : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x →
      ratLeRep x u → ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (hdu : ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep u c))
    (hhe : ratLtRep h eps)
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x c delta) (hcx : ratLeRep c x) :
    close (f x) (f c) eps :=
  close_of_le_ub (f x) (f c) eps h
    (hmono c x
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a c hac) (ratLtRep_le c b hcb))
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_trans a c x (ratLtRep_le a c hac) hcx)
        (ratLeRep_trans x u b
          (ratLeRep_trans x (ratAddRep c delta) u
            (le_add_of_sub_le x c delta
              (And.left (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
            (ratLeRep_trans (ratAddRep c delta)
              (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
              (ratLeRep_add_left delta (ratSubRep u c) c hdu)
              (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
                (ratRel_trans_gen (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c))
                  (ratAddRep (ratSubRep u c) c) u
                  (ratAdd_comm c (ratSubRep u c))
                  (ratSubRep_add_self u c)))))
          hub))
      hcx)
    (hbound x
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_trans a c x (ratLtRep_le a c hac) hcx)
        (ratLeRep_trans x u b
          (ratLeRep_trans x (ratAddRep c delta) u
            (le_add_of_sub_le x c delta
              (And.left (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
            (ratLeRep_trans (ratAddRep c delta)
              (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
              (ratLeRep_add_left delta (ratSubRep u c) c hdu)
              (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
                (ratRel_trans_gen (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c))
                  (ratAddRep (ratSubRep u c) c) u
                  (ratAdd_comm c (ratSubRep u c))
                  (ratSubRep_add_self u c)))))
          hub))
      hcx
      (ratLeRep_trans x (ratAddRep c delta) u
        (le_add_of_sub_le x c delta
          (And.left (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
        (ratLeRep_trans (ratAddRep c delta)
          (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
          (ratLeRep_add_left delta (ratSubRep u c) c hdu)
          (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
            (ratRel_trans_gen (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c))
              (ratAddRep (ratSubRep u c) c) u
              (ratAdd_comm c (ratSubRep u c))
              (ratSubRep_add_self u c))))))
    hhe
练习 9left_close_of_delta定理附录解答

左半邻域上的收口：|x - c| < δ ≤ c - l 时 x 落在 [l, c] 里，于是

fx≤fc≤fx+h<fx+eps
theorem left_close_of_delta (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (a b c l h eps delta : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hal : ratLeRep a l)
    (hbound : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x →
      ratLeRep x c → ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
    (hdl : ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep c l))
    (hhe : ratLtRep h eps)
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x c delta) (hxc : ratLeRep x c) :
    close (f x) (f c) eps :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

从 close_two_bounds 取出 c - x ≤ δ，与 δ ≤ c - l 合成 c - x ≤ c - l，用 le_of_sub_right_le 得 l ≤ x；再交给 close_of_le_lb。

提示 2目标形态

close x c δ → x ≤ c → close (f x) (f c) eps。

提示 3关键件

close_two_bounds · ratLeRep_congr_gen · le_of_sub_right_le · close_of_le_lb。

查看解答 定理 · 45 行

f x ≤ f c ≤ f x + h < f x + eps。

theorem left_close_of_delta (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (a b c l h eps delta : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hal : ratLeRep a l)
    (hbound : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x →
      ratLeRep x c → ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
    (hdl : ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep c l))
    (hhe : ratLtRep h eps)
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x c delta) (hxc : ratLeRep x c) :
    close (f x) (f c) eps :=
  close_of_le_lb (f x) (f c) eps h
    (hmono x c
      (And.intro
        (ratLeRep_trans a l x hal
          (le_of_sub_right_le c x l
            (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep c x) delta (ratSubRep c l)
              (ratLeRep_congr_gen (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) c)
                (ratSubRep c x) delta delta
                (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep x) c) (ratRel_refl delta)
                (And.right (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
              hdl)))
      (ratLeRep_trans x c b hxc (ratLtRep_le c b hcb)))
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a c hac) (ratLtRep_le c b hcb))
      hxc)
    (hbound x
      (And.intro
        (ratLeRep_trans a l x hal
          (le_of_sub_right_le c x l
            (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep c x) delta (ratSubRep c l)
              (ratLeRep_congr_gen (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) c)
                (ratSubRep c x) delta delta
                (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep x) c) (ratRel_refl delta)
                (And.right (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
              hdl)))
      (ratLeRep_trans x c b hxc (ratLtRep_le c b hcb)))
      (le_of_sub_right_le c x l
        (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep c x) delta (ratSubRep c l)
          (ratLeRep_congr_gen (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) c)
            (ratSubRep c x) delta delta
            (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep x) c) (ratRel_refl delta)
            (And.right (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
          hdl))
      hxc)
    hhe
练习 10continuousAt_of_monotone_ivp定理附录解答

主定理。对每个 eps 取 h = eps/2，左右各得一个分界点；δ 取 u - c 与 c - l 中较小的正数，于是 c 的 δ 邻域落在 [l, u] 里。x 在 c 的哪一边由序的全性决定，两边分别交给上面的收口。

theorem continuousAt_of_monotone_ivp (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b) :
    ContinuousAt f c :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

eps 取半；exists_right_bound 与 exists_left_bound 给出 u 与 l；exists_min_pos 取 δ；对 x 用 ratLeRep_total x c 分左右，分别调用 right_close_of_delta 与 left_close_of_delta。

提示 2目标形态

NondecreasingOn f a b → HasIntermediateValue f a b → a < c → c < b → ContinuousAt f c。

提示 3关键件

ratLtRep_half · exists_right_bound · exists_left_bound · exists_min_pos · ratLeRep_total · right_close_of_delta · left_close_of_delta。

查看解答 定理 · 61 行
theorem continuousAt_of_monotone_ivp (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b) :
    ContinuousAt f c :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (u : ratRep) => ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
          ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) (ratHalf eps)))
      (∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x c delta → close (f x) (f c) eps)
      (exists_right_bound f a b c (ratHalf eps) hmono hivp hac hcb
        (ratLtRep_half eps heps))
      (fun (u : ratRep)
          (hu : ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
              ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) (ratHalf eps))) =>
        @Exists.elim ratRep
          (fun (l : ratRep) => ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
              ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) (ratHalf eps)))
          (∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x c delta → close (f x) (f c) eps)
          (exists_left_bound f a b c (ratHalf eps) hmono hivp hac hcb
            (ratLtRep_half eps heps))
          (fun (l : ratRep)
              (hl : ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x →
                  ratLeRep x c → ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) (ratHalf eps))) =>
            @Exists.elim ratRep
              (fun (delta : ratRep) => ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
                ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep u c) ∧
                ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep c l))
              (∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), close x c delta → close (f x) (f c) eps)
              (exists_min_pos (ratSubRep u c) (ratSubRep c l)
                (ratLtRep_sub_pos u c (And.left hu))
                (ratLtRep_sub_pos c l (And.left hl)))
              (fun (delta : ratRep)
                  (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
                    ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep u c) ∧
                    ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep c l)) =>
                Exists.intro delta
                  (And.intro (And.left hd)
                    (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x c delta) =>
                      @Or.elim (ratLeRep x c) (ratLeRep c x)
                        (close (f x) (f c) eps)
                        (fun (hxc : ratLeRep x c) =>
                          left_close_of_delta f a b c l (ratHalf eps) eps delta
                            hmono hac hcb (And.left (And.right hl))
                            (And.right (And.right hl))
                            (And.right (And.right hd))
                            (ratHalf_lt_self eps heps) x hx hxc)
                        (fun (hcx : ratLeRep c x) =>
                          right_close_of_delta f a b c u (ratHalf eps) eps delta
                            hmono hac hcb (And.left (And.right hu))
                            (And.right (And.right hu))
                            (And.left (And.right hd))
                            (ratHalf_lt_self eps heps) x hx hcx)
                        (ratLeRep_total x c))))))

    ∀ (c : ratRep), ratLtRep a c → ratLtRep c b → ContinuousAt f c :=

theorem continuousOn_open_of_monotone_ivp (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b) (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b) :
    ∀ (c : ratRep), ratLtRep a c → ratLtRep c b → ContinuousAt f c :=

theorem demo_ivp_id (a b : ratRep) :
    HasIntermediateValue (fun (x : ratRep) => x) a b :=
  fun (x y : ratRep) (_hx : InClosed a b x) (_hy : InClosed a b y)
      (_hxy : ratLtRep x y) (v : ratRep) (hxv : ratLtRep x v)
      (hvy : ratLtRep v y) =>
    Exists.intro v (And.intro hxv (And.intro hvy (ratRel_refl v)))

theorem demo_continuousAt_id_ivp (a b c : ratRep) (hac : ratLtRep a c)
    (hcb : ratLtRep c b) :
    ContinuousAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) c :=
  continuousAt_of_monotone_ivp (fun (x : ratRep) => x) a b c
    (fun (x y : ratRep) (_hx : InClosed a b x) (_hy : InClosed a b y)
        (hxy : ratLeRep x y) => hxy)
    (demo_ivp_id a b) hac hcb

单调函数：介值性反过来给出连续

练习 1 · strictlyIncreasing_injective 定理
theorem strictlyIncreasing_injective (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (hf : StrictlyIncreasing f) (x y : ratRep) (h : ratRel (f x) (f y)) :
    ratRel x y :=
  @Or.elim (ratLeRep x y) (ratLeRep y x) (ratRel x y)
    (fun (hxy : ratLeRep x y) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel x y) (ratLtRep x y) (ratRel x y)
        (fun (heq : ratRel x y) => heq)
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep x y) =>
          False.elim (ratLtRep_right (f x) (f y) (hf x y hlt) h))
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt x y hxy))
    (fun (hyx : ratLeRep y x) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel y x) (ratLtRep y x) (ratRel x y)
        (fun (heq : ratRel y x) => ratRel_symm y x heq)
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep y x) =>
          False.elim (ratLtRep_right (f y) (f x) (hf y x hlt)
            (ratRel_symm (f x) (f y) h)))
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt y x hyx))
    (ratLeRep_total x y)
练习 2 · right_bound_flat 定理
theorem right_bound_flat (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hfb : ratLeRep (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  Exists.intro b
    (And.intro hcb
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl b)
        (fun (x : ratRep) (hIn : InClosed a b x) (_hcx : ratLeRep c x)
            (hxb : ratLeRep x b) =>
          ratLeRep_trans (f x) (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)
            (hmono x b hIn
              (And.intro
                (ratLtRep_le a b (ratLtRep_trans a c b hac hcb))
                (ratLeRep_refl b))
              hxb)
            hfb)))
练习 3 · right_bound_of_lt 定理
theorem right_bound_of_lt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h)
    (hlt : ratLtRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  @Exists.elim ratRep
    (fun (v : ratRep) => ratLtRep c v ∧ ratLtRep v b ∧
      ratRel (f v) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (hivp c b
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a c hac) (ratLtRep_le c b hcb))
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a b (ratLtRep_trans a c b hac hcb))
        (ratLeRep_refl b))
      hcb (ratAddRep (f c) h)
      (ratLtRep_self_add_pos (f c) h hh) hlt)
    (fun (u : ratRep)
        (hu : ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLtRep u b ∧
          ratRel (f u) (ratAddRep (f c) h)) =>
      Exists.intro u
        (And.intro (And.left hu)
          (And.intro (ratLtRep_le u b (And.left (And.right hu)))
            (fun (x : ratRep) (hIn : InClosed a b x) (hcx : ratLeRep c x)
                (hxu : ratLeRep x u) =>
              ratLeRep_trans (f x) (f u) (ratAddRep (f c) h)
                (hmono x u hIn
                  (And.intro
                    (ratLeRep_trans a c u (ratLtRep_le a c hac)
                      (ratLtRep_le c u (And.left hu)))
                    (ratLtRep_le u b (And.left (And.right hu))))
                  hxu)
                (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (f u) (ratAddRep (f c) h)
                  (And.right (And.right hu)))))))
练习 4 · exists_right_bound 定理
theorem exists_right_bound (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h) :=
  @Or.elim (ratLeRep (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (ratLeRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b))
    (∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
        ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (fun (hle : ratLeRep (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)) =>
      right_bound_flat f a b c h hmono hac hcb hle)
    (fun (hge : ratLeRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b))
        (ratLtRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b))
        (∃ (u : ratRep), ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
            ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
        (fun (heq : ratRel (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) =>
          right_bound_flat f a b c h hmono hac hcb
            (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h)
              (ratRel_symm (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b) heq)))
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b)) =>
          right_bound_of_lt f a b c h hmono hivp hac hcb hh hlt)
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt (ratAddRep (f c) h) (f b) hge))
    (ratLeRep_total (f b) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
练习 5 · left_bound_flat 定理
theorem left_bound_flat (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hfa : ratLeRep (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a)) :
    ∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h) :=
  Exists.intro a
    (And.intro hac
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a)
        (fun (x : ratRep) (hIn : InClosed a b x) (hax : ratLeRep a x)
            (hxc : ratLeRep x c) =>
          ratLeRep_congr_gen (f c) (f c)
            (ratAddRep h (f x)) (ratAddRep (f x) h)
            (ratRel_refl (f c)) (ratAdd_comm h (f x))
            (le_add_of_sub_le (f c) h (f x)
              (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a) (f x) hfa
                (hmono a x
                  (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a)
                    (ratLeRep_trans a c b (ratLtRep_le a c hac)
                      (ratLtRep_le c b hcb)))
                  hIn hax))))))
练习 6 · left_bound_of_lt 定理
theorem left_bound_of_lt (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h)
    (hlt : ratLtRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) :
    ∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h) :=
  @Exists.elim ratRep
    (fun (v : ratRep) => ratLtRep a v ∧ ratLtRep v c ∧
      ratRel (f v) (ratSubRep (f c) h))
    (∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
    (hivp a c
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_refl a)
        (ratLtRep_le a b (ratLtRep_trans a c b hac hcb)))
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a c hac) (ratLtRep_le c b hcb))
      hac (ratSubRep (f c) h) hlt (ratLtRep_self_sub_pos (f c) h hh))
    (fun (l : ratRep)
        (hl : ratLtRep a l ∧ ratLtRep l c ∧
          ratRel (f l) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) =>
      Exists.intro l
        (And.intro (And.left (And.right hl))
          (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a l (And.left hl))
            (fun (x : ratRep) (hIn : InClosed a b x) (hlx : ratLeRep l x)
                (hxc : ratLeRep x c) =>
              ratLeRep_congr_gen (f c) (f c)
                (ratAddRep h (f x)) (ratAddRep (f x) h)
                (ratRel_refl (f c)) (ratAdd_comm h (f x))
                (le_add_of_sub_le (f c) h (f x)
                  (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f l) (f x)
                    (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f l)
                      (ratRel_symm (f l) (ratSubRep (f c) h)
                        (And.right (And.right hl))))
                    (hmono l x
                      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a l (And.left hl))
                        (ratLeRep_trans l c b
                          (ratLtRep_le l c (And.left (And.right hl)))
                          (ratLtRep_le c b hcb)))
                      hIn hlx)))))))
练习 7 · exists_left_bound 定理
theorem exists_left_bound (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c h : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hh : ratLtRep ratZeroRep h) :
    ∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h) :=
  @Or.elim (ratLeRep (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a))
    (ratLeRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h))
    (∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
      ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
        ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
    (fun (hfa : ratLeRep (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a)) =>
      left_bound_flat f a b c h hmono hac hcb hfa)
    (fun (haf : ratLeRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) =>
      @Or.elim (ratRel (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h))
        (ratLtRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h))
        (∃ (l : ratRep), ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
          ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
            ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
        (fun (heq : ratRel (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) =>
          left_bound_flat f a b c h hmono hac hcb
            (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a)
              (ratRel_symm (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h) heq)))
        (fun (hlt : ratLtRep (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h)) =>
          left_bound_of_lt f a b c h hmono hivp hac hcb hh hlt)
        (ratLeRep_eq_or_lt (f a) (ratSubRep (f c) h) haf))
    (ratLeRep_total (ratSubRep (f c) h) (f a))
练习 8 · right_close_of_delta 定理
theorem right_close_of_delta (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (a b c u h eps delta : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hub : ratLeRep u b)
    (hbound : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x →
      ratLeRep x u → ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) h))
    (hdu : ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep u c))
    (hhe : ratLtRep h eps)
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x c delta) (hcx : ratLeRep c x) :
    close (f x) (f c) eps :=
  close_of_le_ub (f x) (f c) eps h
    (hmono c x
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a c hac) (ratLtRep_le c b hcb))
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_trans a c x (ratLtRep_le a c hac) hcx)
        (ratLeRep_trans x u b
          (ratLeRep_trans x (ratAddRep c delta) u
            (le_add_of_sub_le x c delta
              (And.left (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
            (ratLeRep_trans (ratAddRep c delta)
              (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
              (ratLeRep_add_left delta (ratSubRep u c) c hdu)
              (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
                (ratRel_trans_gen (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c))
                  (ratAddRep (ratSubRep u c) c) u
                  (ratAdd_comm c (ratSubRep u c))
                  (ratSubRep_add_self u c)))))
          hub))
      hcx)
    (hbound x
      (And.intro (ratLeRep_trans a c x (ratLtRep_le a c hac) hcx)
        (ratLeRep_trans x u b
          (ratLeRep_trans x (ratAddRep c delta) u
            (le_add_of_sub_le x c delta
              (And.left (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
            (ratLeRep_trans (ratAddRep c delta)
              (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
              (ratLeRep_add_left delta (ratSubRep u c) c hdu)
              (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
                (ratRel_trans_gen (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c))
                  (ratAddRep (ratSubRep u c) c) u
                  (ratAdd_comm c (ratSubRep u c))
                  (ratSubRep_add_self u c)))))
          hub))
      hcx
      (ratLeRep_trans x (ratAddRep c delta) u
        (le_add_of_sub_le x c delta
          (And.left (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
        (ratLeRep_trans (ratAddRep c delta)
          (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
          (ratLeRep_add_left delta (ratSubRep u c) c hdu)
          (ratLeRep_of_ratRel (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c)) u
            (ratRel_trans_gen (ratAddRep c (ratSubRep u c))
              (ratAddRep (ratSubRep u c) c) u
              (ratAdd_comm c (ratSubRep u c))
              (ratSubRep_add_self u c))))))
    hhe
练习 9 · left_close_of_delta 定理

f x ≤ f c ≤ f x + h < f x + eps。

theorem left_close_of_delta (f : ratRep → ratRep)
    (a b c l h eps delta : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b)
    (hal : ratLeRep a l)
    (hbound : ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x →
      ratLeRep x c → ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) h))
    (hdl : ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep c l))
    (hhe : ratLtRep h eps)
    (x : ratRep) (hx : close x c delta) (hxc : ratLeRep x c) :
    close (f x) (f c) eps :=
  close_of_le_lb (f x) (f c) eps h
    (hmono x c
      (And.intro
        (ratLeRep_trans a l x hal
          (le_of_sub_right_le c x l
            (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep c x) delta (ratSubRep c l)
              (ratLeRep_congr_gen (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) c)
                (ratSubRep c x) delta delta
                (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep x) c) (ratRel_refl delta)
                (And.right (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
              hdl)))
      (ratLeRep_trans x c b hxc (ratLtRep_le c b hcb)))
      (And.intro (ratLtRep_le a c hac) (ratLtRep_le c b hcb))
      hxc)
    (hbound x
      (And.intro
        (ratLeRep_trans a l x hal
          (le_of_sub_right_le c x l
            (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep c x) delta (ratSubRep c l)
              (ratLeRep_congr_gen (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) c)
                (ratSubRep c x) delta delta
                (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep x) c) (ratRel_refl delta)
                (And.right (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
              hdl)))
      (ratLeRep_trans x c b hxc (ratLtRep_le c b hcb)))
      (le_of_sub_right_le c x l
        (ratLeRep_trans (ratSubRep c x) delta (ratSubRep c l)
          (ratLeRep_congr_gen (ratAddRep (ratNegRep x) c)
            (ratSubRep c x) delta delta
            (ratAdd_comm (ratNegRep x) c) (ratRel_refl delta)
            (And.right (close_two_bounds x c delta hx)))
          hdl))
      hxc)
    hhe
练习 10 · continuousAt_of_monotone_ivp 定理
theorem continuousAt_of_monotone_ivp (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b c : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b)
    (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b)
    (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b) :
    ContinuousAt f c :=
  fun (eps : ratRep) (heps : ratLtRep ratZeroRep eps) =>
    @Exists.elim ratRep
      (fun (u : ratRep) => ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
          ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) (ratHalf eps)))
      (∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
        ∀ (x : ratRep), close x c delta → close (f x) (f c) eps)
      (exists_right_bound f a b c (ratHalf eps) hmono hivp hac hcb
        (ratLtRep_half eps heps))
      (fun (u : ratRep)
          (hu : ratLtRep c u ∧ ratLeRep u b ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep c x → ratLeRep x u →
              ratLeRep (f x) (ratAddRep (f c) (ratHalf eps))) =>
        @Exists.elim ratRep
          (fun (l : ratRep) => ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x → ratLeRep x c →
              ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) (ratHalf eps)))
          (∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
            ∀ (x : ratRep), close x c delta → close (f x) (f c) eps)
          (exists_left_bound f a b c (ratHalf eps) hmono hivp hac hcb
            (ratLtRep_half eps heps))
          (fun (l : ratRep)
              (hl : ratLtRep l c ∧ ratLeRep a l ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), InClosed a b x → ratLeRep l x →
                  ratLeRep x c → ratLeRep (f c) (ratAddRep (f x) (ratHalf eps))) =>
            @Exists.elim ratRep
              (fun (delta : ratRep) => ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
                ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep u c) ∧
                ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep c l))
              (∃ (delta : ratRep), ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
                ∀ (x : ratRep), close x c delta → close (f x) (f c) eps)
              (exists_min_pos (ratSubRep u c) (ratSubRep c l)
                (ratLtRep_sub_pos u c (And.left hu))
                (ratLtRep_sub_pos c l (And.left hl)))
              (fun (delta : ratRep)
                  (hd : ratLtRep ratZeroRep delta ∧
                    ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep u c) ∧
                    ratLeRep delta (ratSubRep c l)) =>
                Exists.intro delta
                  (And.intro (And.left hd)
                    (fun (x : ratRep) (hx : close x c delta) =>
                      @Or.elim (ratLeRep x c) (ratLeRep c x)
                        (close (f x) (f c) eps)
                        (fun (hxc : ratLeRep x c) =>
                          left_close_of_delta f a b c l (ratHalf eps) eps delta
                            hmono hac hcb (And.left (And.right hl))
                            (And.right (And.right hl))
                            (And.right (And.right hd))
                            (ratHalf_lt_self eps heps) x hx hxc)
                        (fun (hcx : ratLeRep c x) =>
                          right_close_of_delta f a b c u (ratHalf eps) eps delta
                            hmono hac hcb (And.left (And.right hu))
                            (And.right (And.right hu))
                            (And.left (And.right hd))
                            (ratHalf_lt_self eps heps) x hx hcx)
                        (ratLeRep_total x c))))))
练习 11 · continuousOn_open_of_monotone_ivp 定理
theorem continuousOn_open_of_monotone_ivp (f : ratRep → ratRep) (a b : ratRep)
    (hmono : NondecreasingOn f a b) (hivp : HasIntermediateValue f a b) :
    ∀ (c : ratRep), ratLtRep a c → ratLtRep c b → ContinuousAt f c :=
  fun (c : ratRep) (hac : ratLtRep a c) (hcb : ratLtRep c b) =>
    continuousAt_of_monotone_ivp f a b c hmono hivp hac hcb
练习 12 · demo_ivp_id 定理
theorem demo_ivp_id (a b : ratRep) :
    HasIntermediateValue (fun (x : ratRep) => x) a b :=
  fun (x y : ratRep) (_hx : InClosed a b x) (_hy : InClosed a b y)
      (_hxy : ratLtRep x y) (v : ratRep) (hxv : ratLtRep x v)
      (hvy : ratLtRep v y) =>
    Exists.intro v (And.intro hxv (And.intro hvy (ratRel_refl v)))
练习 13 · demo_continuousAt_id_ivp 定理
theorem demo_continuousAt_id_ivp (a b c : ratRep) (hac : ratLtRep a c)
    (hcb : ratLtRep c b) :
    ContinuousAt (fun (x : ratRep) => x) c :=
  continuousAt_of_monotone_ivp (fun (x : ratRep) => x) a b c
    (fun (x y : ratRep) (_hx : InClosed a b x) (_hy : InClosed a b y)
        (hxy : ratLeRep x y) => hxy)
    (demo_ivp_id a b) hac hcb
